1→"""Internal sync helpers for auto-cluster regeneration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_strategy import (
     9→    cluster_name_from_key as _cluster_name_from_key,
    10→    generate_action as _generate_action,
    11→    generate_description as _generate_description,
    12→    grouping_key as _grouping_key,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    15→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    16→    _current_stale_ids,
    17→    current_under_target_ids,
    18→    current_unscored_ids,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    21→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    22→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    27→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    28→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    29→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    30→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    31→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    32→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    33→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    34→
    35→
    36→def _sync_auto_cluster(
    37→    plan: dict,
    38→    clusters: dict,
    39→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
    40→    *,
    41→    cluster_key: str,
    42→    cluster_name: str,
    43→    member_ids: list[str],
    44→    description: str,
    45→    action: str,
    46→    now: str,
    47→    optional: bool = False,
    48→) -> int:
    49→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries."""
    50→    changes = 0
    51→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
    52→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
    53→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    54→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    55→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
    56→        if (
    57→            old_ids != new_ids_set
    58→            or cluster.get("description") != description
    59→            or cluster.get("action") != action
    60→        ):
    61→            cluster["issue_ids"] = list(member_ids)
    62→            cluster["description"] = description
    63→            cluster["action"] = action
    64→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
    65→            changes = 1
    66→    else:
    67→        new_cluster = {
    68→            "name": cluster_name,
    69→            "description": description,
    70→            "issue_ids": list(member_ids),
    71→            "created_at": now,
    72→            "updated_at": now,
    73→            "auto": True,
    74→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
    75→            "action": action,
    76→            "user_modified": False,
    77→        }
    78→        if optional:
    79→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
    80→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
    81→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
    82→        changes = 1
    83→
    84→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    85→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
    86→    for fid in member_ids:
    87→        if fid not in overrides:
    88→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    89→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
    90→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
    91→
    92→    return changes
    93→
    94→
    95→def sync_issue_clusters(
    96→    plan: dict,
    97→    issues: dict,
    98→    clusters: dict,
    99→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   100→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   101→    now: str,
   102→) -> int:
   103→    """Group open issues by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   104→    changes = 0
   105→
   106→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   108→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   109→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   110→
   111→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   112→    issue_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   113→    for fid, issue in issues.items():
   114→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   115→            continue
   116→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   117→            continue
   118→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   119→            continue
   120→
   121→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   122→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   123→        key = _grouping_key(issue, meta)
   124→        if key is None:
   125→            continue
   126→
   127→        groups[key].append(fid)
   128→        issue_data[fid] = issue
   129→
   130→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   131→
   132→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   133→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   134→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   135→
   136→        rep = issue_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   137→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   138→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→        members = [issue_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in issue_data]
   140→
   141→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   142→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   143→
   144→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   145→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   146→
   147→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   148→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   149→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   150→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   151→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   152→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   153→                if new_ids:
   154→                    cluster["issue_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   155→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   156→                    changes += 1
   157→                overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   158→                for fid in member_ids:
   159→                    if fid not in overrides:
   160→                        overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   161→                    overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   162→                    overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   163→                continue
   164→
   165→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   166→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   167→
   168→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   169→            plan,
   170→            clusters,
   171→            existing_by_key,
   172→            cluster_key=key,
   173→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   174→            member_ids=member_ids,
   175→            description=description,
   176→            action=action,
   177→            now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    return changes
   181→
   182→
   183→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   184→    plan: dict,
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    issues: dict,
   187→    clusters: dict,
   188→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   189→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   190→    now: str,
   191→    *,
   192→    target_strict: float,
   193→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   194→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   195→) -> int:
   196→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   197→    changes = 0
   198→
   199→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   201→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    if policy is not None:
   205→        unscored_state_ids = policy.unscored_ids
   206→        stale_state_ids = policy.stale_ids
   207→    else:
   208→        unscored_state_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   209→        stale_state_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   210→
   211→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   212→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   213→    )
   214→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   215→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   216→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   217→    )
   218→
   219→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   220→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   221→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   222→        description = (
   223→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   224→        )
   225→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   226→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   227→            plan,
   228→            clusters,
   229→            existing_by_key,
   230→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   231→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   232→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   233→            description=description,
   234→            action=action,
   235→            now=now,
   236→        )
   237→
   238→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   239→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   240→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   241→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   242→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   243→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   244→            plan,
   245→            clusters,
   246→            existing_by_key,
   247→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   248→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   249→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   250→            description=description,
   251→            action=action,
   252→            now=now,
   253→        )
   254→
   255→    if policy is not None:
   256→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
   257→    else:
   258→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   259→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   260→
   261→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   262→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   263→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   264→    ut_prune = [
   265→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   266→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   267→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   268→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   269→        and fid in order
   270→    ]
   271→    for fid in ut_prune:
   272→        order.remove(fid)
   273→        changes += 1
   274→
   275→    if policy is not None:
   276→        has_objective_items = policy.has_objective_backlog
   277→    else:
   278→        has_objective_items = any(
   279→            f.get("status") == "open"
   280→            and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   281→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   282→            for f in issues.values()
   283→        )
   284→
   285→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   286→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   287→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   288→        description = (
   289→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   290→            f"dimensions under target score"
   291→        )
   292→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   293→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   294→            plan,
   295→            clusters,
   296→            existing_by_key,
   297→            [REDACTED],
   298→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   299→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   300→            description=description,
   301→            action=action,
   302→            now=now,
   303→            optional=True,
   304→        )
   305→
   306→        existing_order = set(order)
   307→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   308→            if fid not in existing_order:
   309→                order.append(fid)
   310→
   311→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   312→        objective_evict = [
   313→            fid for fid in order
   314→            if fid in under_target_ids
   315→        ]
   316→        for fid in objective_evict:
   317→            order.remove(fid)
   318→            changes += 1
   319→
   320→    return changes
   321→
   322→
   323→def prune_stale_clusters(
   324→    plan: dict,
   325→    issues: dict,
   326→    clusters: dict,
   327→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   328→    now: str,
   329→) -> int:
   330→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   331→    changes = 0
   332→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   333→        cluster = clusters[name]
   334→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   335→            continue
   336→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   337→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   338→            continue
   339→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   340→            alive = [
   341→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   342→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   343→            ]
   344→            if alive:
   345→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])):
   346→                    cluster["issue_ids"] = alive
   347→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   348→                    changes += 1
   349→                continue
   350→        del clusters[name]
   351→        for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   352→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   353→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   354→                override["cluster"] = None
   355→                override["updated_at"] = now
   356→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   357→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   358→        changes += 1
   359→    return changes
   360→
   361→
   362→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   363→
```

    41→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    43→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    44→    state = runtime.state
    45→
    46→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    47→        return
    48→
    49→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    50→    if config_warning:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    54→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    55→
    56→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    57→    if output:
    58→        try:
    59→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    60→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    61→            print_agent_plan(
    62→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    63→                next_command=next_command,
    64→            )
    65→        except OSError as e:
    66→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    67→    else:
    68→        print(plan_md)
    69→        print()
    70→        print_agent_plan(
    71→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    72→            next_command=next_command,
    73→        )
    74→
    75→
    76→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    77→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    78→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    79→

    81→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    82→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    83→    plan = load_plan()
    84→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    85→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    86→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    87→    kind_counts = {
    88→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    89→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    90→    }
    91→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    92→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    93→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    94→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    95→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    96→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    97→
    98→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
    99→
   100→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   101→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   102→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   103→    if total_skipped:
   104→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   105→    else:
   106→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   107→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   108→    if clusters:
   109→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   110→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   111→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   112→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   113→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   114→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   115→    if described or noted:
   116→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   117→    if active:
   118→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   119→    if superseded:
   120→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   121→
   122→    # Commit tracking summary
   123→    _cfg = load_config()
   124→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   125→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   126→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   127→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   128→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   129→            print(
   130→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   131→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   132→            )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   136→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   137→    plan = load_plan()
   138→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   139→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   140→    reset_plan(plan)
   141→    append_log_entry(
   142→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   143→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   144→    )
   145→    save_plan(plan)
   146→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   147→
   148→
   149→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   150→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   151→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   152→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   153→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   154→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   155→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   156→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   157→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   158→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   159→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   160→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   161→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   162→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   163→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   164→}
   165→
   166→
   167→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   168→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   169→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   170→    if plan_action is None:
   171→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   175→    if handler is None:
   176→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   177→        return
   178→    handler(args)
   179→
   180→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import re
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.reorder_handlers import resolve_target
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    14→    add_to_cluster,
    15→    append_log_entry,
    16→    create_cluster,
    17→    delete_cluster,
    18→    load_plan,
    19→    merge_clusters,
    20→    move_items,
    21→    remove_from_cluster,
    22→    save_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    25→
    26→_LEADING_NUM_RE = re.compile(r'^\d+\.\s*')
    27→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
    28→
    29→
    30→def _suggest_close_matches(state: dict, plan: dict | None, patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    31→    """Print fuzzy match suggestions for patterns that resolved to zero issues."""
    32→    all_ids: list[str] = list(state.get("issues", {}).keys())
    33→    if plan is not None:
    34→        seen_ids: set[str] = set(all_ids)
    35→        for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→            if fid not in seen_ids:
    37→                seen_ids.add(fid)
    38→                all_ids.append(fid)
    39→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    40→            for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    41→                if fid not in seen_ids:
    42→                    seen_ids.add(fid)
    43→                    all_ids.append(fid)
    44→
    45→    for pattern in patterns:
    46→        segments = pattern.split("::")
    47→        last_seg = segments[-1]
    48→        suggestions: list[str] = []
    49→
    50→        if _HEX8_RE.match(last_seg):
    51→            suggestions = [fid for fid in all_ids if fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}") or fid == last_seg]
    52→            if suggestions:
    53→                print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    54→                print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    55→                for m in suggestions[:3]:
    56→                    print(colorize(f"    {m}", "dim"))
    57→                print(colorize(f"  Tip: match by hash suffix alone: {last_seg}", "dim"))
    58→            continue
    59→
    60→        # Try last segment and second-to-last as descriptive name slugs
    61→        slug = segments[-2] if len(segments) >= 2 else ""
    62→        for fid in all_ids:
    63→            if f"::{last_seg}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}"):
    64→                suggestions.append(fid)
    65→            elif slug and (f"::{slug}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{slug}")):
    66→                suggestions.append(fid)
    67→        if suggestions:
    68→            print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    69→            print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    70→            for m in suggestions[:3]:
    71→                print(colorize(f"    {m}", "dim"))
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:
    75→    """Print valid pattern format hints after a no-match error.
    76→
    77→    Side-effect only: prints help text to stdout when pattern resolution
    78→    returns zero matches, guiding the user toward valid pattern syntax.
    79→    """
    80→    print(colorize("  Valid patterns:", "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    f41b3eb7              (8-char hash suffix from dashboard)", "dim"))
    82→    print(colorize("    review::path::name    (ID prefix)", "dim"))
    83→    print(colorize("    review                (all issues from detector)", "dim"))
    84→    print(colorize("    src/foo.py            (all issues in file)", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize("    timing_attack         (issue name — last ::segment of ID)", "dim"))
    86→    print(colorize("    review::*naming*      (glob pattern)", "dim"))
    87→    print(colorize("    my-cluster            (cluster name — expands to members)", "dim"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    91→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    92→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    93→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    94→    plan = load_plan()
    95→    try:
    96→        create_cluster(plan, name, description, action=action)
    97→    except ValueError as ex:
    98→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    99→        return
   100→    append_log_entry(
   101→        plan, "cluster_create", cluster_name=name, actor="user",
   102→        detail={"description": description, "action": action},
   103→    )
   104→    save_plan(plan)
   105→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   110→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   111→        return
   112→
   113→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   114→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   115→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   116→
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   119→    if not issue_ids:
   120→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   121→        _print_pattern_hints()
   122→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   123→        return
   124→
   125→    if dry_run:
   126→        print(colorize(f"  [dry-run] Would add {len(issue_ids)} item(s) to {cluster_name}:", "cyan"))
   127→        for fid in issue_ids:
   128→            print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    try:
   132→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)
   133→    except ValueError as ex:
   134→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    # Check for overlap with other manual clusters
   138→    member_set = set(issue_ids)
   139→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   140→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
   141→            continue
   142→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   143→        if not other_ids:
   144→            continue
   145→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
   146→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
   147→            print(colorize(
   148→                f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} issue(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' "
   149→                f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {int(len(overlap)/len(other_ids)*100)}% overlap).",
   150→                "yellow",
   151→            ))
   152→
   153→    append_log_entry(
   154→        plan, "cluster_add", issue_ids=issue_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   155→    )
   156→    save_plan(plan)
   157→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   158→
   159→

   165→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")

   173→        _print_pattern_hints()
   174→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)

   209→
   210→def _cmd_cluster_reorder(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    raw_names: str = getattr(args, "cluster_names", "") or getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   212→    cluster_names: list[str] = [n.strip() for n in raw_names.split(",") if n.strip()]
   213→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
   214→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
   215→    item_pattern: str | None = getattr(args, "item_pattern", None)
   216→
   217→    plan = load_plan()
   218→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   219→
   220→    # Validate all names exist
   221→    for name in cluster_names:
   222→        if name not in clusters:
   223→            print(colorize(f"  Cluster {name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   224→            return
   225→
   226→    if item_pattern is not None:
   227→        # Within-cluster item reorder
   228→        if len(cluster_names) != 1:
   229→            print(colorize("  --item requires exactly one cluster name.", "red"))
   230→            return
   231→        cluster_name = cluster_names[0]
   232→        cluster_member_set = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids", []))
   233→
   234→        state = command_runtime(args).state
   235→        item_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, [item_pattern], plan=plan)
   236→        if not item_ids:
   237→            print(colorize("  No matching issues found for --item pattern.", "yellow"))
   238→            return
   239→        for fid in item_ids:
   240→            if fid not in cluster_member_set:
   241→                print(colorize(f"  {fid!r} is not a member of cluster {cluster_name!r}.", "red"))
   242→                return
   243→
   244→        queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   245→        ordered_slice = [fid for fid in queue_order if fid in cluster_member_set]
   246→
   247→        offset: int | None = None
   248→        resolved_target: str | None = None
   249→        item_set = set(item_ids)
   250→
   251→        if position == "top":
   252→            first_non_item = next((fid for fid in ordered_slice if fid not in item_set), None)
   253→            if first_non_item is None or set(ordered_slice[:len(item_ids)]) == item_set:
   254→                print(colorize("  Already at the top of the cluster.", "yellow"))
   255→                return
   256→            resolved_target = first_non_item
   257→            position = "before"
   258→        elif position == "bottom":
   259→            last_non_item = next((fid for fid in reversed(ordered_slice) if fid not in item_set), None)
   260→            if last_non_item is None or set(ordered_slice[-len(item_ids):]) == item_set:
   261→                print(colorize("  Already at the bottom of the cluster.", "yellow"))
   262→                return
   263→            resolved_target = last_non_item
   264→            position = "after"
   265→        elif position in ("before", "after"):
   266→            if target is None:
   267→                print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires a target. Example: --item PAT {position} TARGET", "red"))
   268→                return
   269→            target_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, [target], plan=plan)
   270→            if not target_ids:
   271→                print(colorize(f"  No match for target {target!r}.", "yellow"))
   272→                return
   273→            resolved_target = target_ids[0]
   274→            if resolved_target not in cluster_member_set:
   275→                print(colorize(f"  Target {resolved_target!r} is not in cluster {cluster_name!r}.", "red"))
   276→                return
   277→        elif position in ("up", "down"):
   278→            if target is None:
   279→                print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires an offset. Example: --item PAT {position} 3", "red"))
   280→                return
   281→            try:
   282→                offset = int(target)
   283→            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   284→                print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   285→                return
   286→
   287→        count = move_items(plan, item_ids, position, target=resolved_target, offset=offset)
   288→        append_log_entry(
   289→            plan, "cluster_reorder", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   290→            detail={"position": position, "count": count, "item": item_pattern},
   291→        )
   292→        save_plan(plan)
   293→        print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position} within cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    # Whole-cluster block reorder (original behaviour)
   297→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
   298→
   299→    offset = None
   300→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   301→        try:
   302→            offset = int(target)
   303→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   304→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   305→            return
   306→        target = None
   307→
   308→    seen: set[str] = set()
   309→    all_member_ids: list[str] = []
   310→    for name in cluster_names:
   311→        for fid in clusters[name].get("issue_ids", []):
   312→            if fid not in seen:
   313→                seen.add(fid)
   314→                all_member_ids.append(fid)
   315→
   316→    if not all_member_ids:
   317→        print(colorize("  No members in the specified cluster(s).", "yellow"))
   318→        return
   319→
   320→    count = move_items(plan, all_member_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   321→    append_log_entry(
   322→        plan, "cluster_reorder", cluster_name=",".join(cluster_names), actor="user",
   323→        detail={"position": position, "count": count},
   324→    )
   325→    save_plan(plan)
   326→    label = ", ".join(cluster_names)
   327→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster(s) {label} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   328→
   329→
   330→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, issue: dict | None) -> None:
   331→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional issue details."""
   332→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   333→    if not issue:
   334→        return
   335→    file = issue.get("file", "")
   336→    lines = issue.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   337→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   338→    if file:
   339→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   340→    summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   341→    if summary:
   342→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   343→

   345→def _load_issues_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   346→    """Load issues from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   347→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   348→    return rt.state.get("issues", {})
   349→

   351→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   352→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   353→    plan = load_plan()
   354→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   355→    if cluster is None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Header
   360→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   361→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   362→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   363→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   364→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   365→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   366→    if desc:
   367→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   368→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   369→    if action:
   370→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   371→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   372→    if steps:
   373→        print(colorize(f"  Steps ({len(steps)}):", "dim"))
   374→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   375→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
   376→
   377→    # Members
   378→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   379→    if not issue_ids:
   380→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   381→    else:
   382→        issues = _load_issues_best_effort(args)
   383→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(issue_ids)}):", "dim"))
   384→        for idx, fid in enumerate(issue_ids, 1):
   385→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, issues.get(fid))
   386→
   387→    # Commands
   388→    print()
   389→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   390→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   391→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   392→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   393→
   394→
   395→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   396→    plan = load_plan()
   397→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   398→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   399→    verbose: bool = getattr(args, "verbose", False)
   400→
   401→    if not clusters:
   402→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   403→        return
   404→
   405→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   406→    pos_map = {fid: i for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order)}
   407→
   408→    def _min_pos(cluster_data: dict) -> int:
   409→        positions = [pos_map[fid] for fid in cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []) if fid in pos_map]
   410→        return min(positions) if positions else 999_999
   411→
   412→    min_pos_cache = {name: _min_pos(c) for name, c in clusters.items()}
   413→    sorted_clusters = sorted(clusters.items(), key=lambda kv: min_pos_cache[kv[0]])
   414→
   415→    if verbose:
   416→        name_width = max(20, min(35, max(len(n) for n, _ in sorted_clusters)))
   417→        total = len(sorted_clusters)
   418→        print(colorize(f"  Clusters ({total} total, sorted by queue position):", "bold"))
   419→        print()
   420→        header = f"  {'#pos':<5}  {'Name':<{name_width}}  {'Items':>5}  {'Steps':>5}  {'Type':<6}  Description"
   421→        sep = f"  {'─'*4}  {'─'*name_width}  {'─'*5}  {'─'*5}  {'─'*6}  {'─'*45}"
   422→        print(colorize(header, "dim"))
   423→        print(colorize(sep, "dim"))
   424→        for name, cluster in sorted_clusters:
   425→            min_p = min_pos_cache[name]
   426→            pos_str = f"#{min_p}" if min_p < 999_999 else "—"
   427→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   428→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   429→            steps_str = str(len(steps)) if steps else "—"
   430→            type_str = "auto" if cluster.get("auto") else "manual"
   431→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   432→            if not desc and min_p == 999_999 and not member_count:
   433→                desc = "(no queue position — no members)"
   434→            desc_truncated = (desc[:44] + "…") if len(desc) > 45 else desc
   435→            name_display = (name[:name_width - 1] + "…") if len(name) > name_width else name
   436→            focused = " *" if name == active else ""
   437→            print(f"  {pos_str:>5}  {name_display:<{name_width}}  {member_count:>5}  {steps_str:>5}  {type_str:<6}  {desc_truncated}{focused}")
   438→        print()
   439→        return
   440→
   441→    print(colorize("  Clusters (ordered by queue position):", "bold"))
   442→    for name, cluster in sorted_clusters:
   443→        min_p = min_pos_cache[name]
   444→        pos_str = f"#{min_p}" if min_p < 999_999 else "—"
   445→        member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   446→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   447→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   448→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   449→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   450→        print(f"    {pos_str:>5}  {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   451→
   452→
   453→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   454→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   455→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   456→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   457→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   458→
   459→    if description is None and steps is None:
   460→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   461→        return
   462→
   463→    plan = load_plan()
   464→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   465→    if cluster is None:
   466→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   467→        return
   468→
   469→    if description is not None:
   470→        cluster["description"] = description
   471→    if steps is not None:
   472→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   473→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   474→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   475→            clean = _LEADING_NUM_RE.sub('', step)
   476→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {clean}", "dim"))
   477→        if len(steps) == 0:
   478→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   479→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   480→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   481→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   482→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   483→    append_log_entry(
   484→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   485→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   486→    )
   487→    save_plan(plan)
   488→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   489→
   490→
   491→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   492→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   493→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   494→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   495→
   496→    plan = load_plan()
   497→    try:
   498→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   499→    except ValueError as ex:
   500→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   501→        return
   502→
   503→    append_log_entry(
   504→        plan, "cluster_merge", issue_ids=source_ids,
   505→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   506→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   507→    )
   508→    save_plan(plan)
   509→    print(colorize(
   510→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   511→        f"{added} issue(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   512→        "green",
   513→    ))
   514→
   515→
   516→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   517→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   518→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   519→    dispatch = {
   520→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   521→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   522→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   523→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   524→        "reorder": _cmd_cluster_reorder,
   525→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   526→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   527→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   528→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   529→    }
   530→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   531→    if handler is None:
   532→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   533→        return
   534→    handler(args)
   535→
   536→
   537→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   538→

1→"""Unused declarations detection via tsc TS6133/TS6192.
     2→
     3→Includes a Deno/edge-functions fallback where `tsc` cannot model URL-based imports.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import argparse
     7→import json
     8→import logging
     9→import re
    10→import subprocess
    11→import sys
    12→from collections import defaultdict
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import (
    16→
    17→    rel,
    18→
    19→    resolve_path,
    20→
    21→    safe_write_text,
    22→
    23→)
    24→
    25→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import (
    26→
    27→    find_ts_files,
    28→
    29→    read_file_text,
    30→
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, print_table
    33→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    34→from desloppify.base.text_utils import strip_c_style_comments
    35→
    36→TS6133_RE = re.compile(
    37→    r"^(.+)\((\d+),(\d+)\): error TS6133: '(\S+)' is declared but its value is never read\."
    38→)
    39→TS6192_RE = re.compile(
    40→    r"^(.+)\((\d+),(\d+)\): error TS6192: All imports in import declaration are unused\."
    41→)
    42→_IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*$")
    43→_DENO_IMPORT_RE = re.compile(
    44→    r"""(?:from\s+['"](?:https://(?:deno\.land|esm\.sh)|npm:|jsr:)|^\s*import\s+['"](?:https://(?:deno\.land|esm\.sh)|npm:|jsr:))"""
    45→)
    46→_DECL_RE = re.compile(
    47→    r"^\s*(export\s+)?(?:const|let|var|function|class)\s+([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*)\b"
    48→)
    49→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _identifier_occurrences(content: str, name: str) -> int:
    53→    pat = re.compile(rf"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_$]){re.escape(name)}(?![A-Za-z0-9_$])")
    54→    return len(pat.findall(content))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _extract_import_names(line: str) -> list[str]:
    58→    """Return local identifier names declared by an import line."""
    59→    if " from " not in line:
    60→        return []
    61→
    62→    left = line.split(" from ", 1)[0].strip()
    63→    if not left.startswith("import "):
    64→        return []
    65→
    66→    clause = left[len("import ") :].strip()
    67→    if clause.startswith("type "):
    68→        clause = clause[len("type ") :].strip()
    69→    if not clause:
    70→        return []
    71→
    72→    names: list[str] = []
    73→    star_match = re.search(r"\*\s+as\s+([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*)", clause)
    74→    if star_match:
    75→        names.append(star_match.group(1))
    76→
    77→    # Default import (e.g., import Foo, {bar} from "x")
    78→    default_part = clause.split(",", 1)[0].strip()
    79→    if default_part and not default_part.startswith("{") and not default_part.startswith(
    80→        "*"
    81→    ):
    82→        if _IDENT_RE.match(default_part):
    83→            names.append(default_part)
    84→
    85→    # Named imports
    86→    for block in re.findall(r"\{([^}]*)\}", clause):
    87→        for item in block.split(","):
    88→            token = item.strip()
    89→            if not token:
    90→                continue
    91→            if token.startswith("type "):
    92→                token = token[len("type ") :].strip()
    93→            alias = re.split(r"\s+as\s+", token)
    94→            local_name = alias[-1].strip()
    95→            if _IDENT_RE.match(local_name):
    96→                names.append(local_name)
    97→
    98→    # Preserve stable order while deduping.
    99→    deduped: list[str] = []
   100→    seen: set[str] = set()
   101→    for name in names:
   102→        if name in seen:
   103→            continue
   104→        seen.add(name)
   105→        deduped.append(name)
   106→    return deduped
   107→
   108→
   109→def _detect_unused_fallback(path: Path, category: str) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   110→    """Conservative source-based fallback for Deno/edge TS projects."""
   111→    files = find_ts_files(path)
   112→    entries: list[dict] = []
   113→
   114→    for filepath in files:
   115→        full = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
   116→        raw = read_file_text(str(full))
   117→        if raw is None:
   118→            continue
   119→        code = strip_c_style_comments(raw)
   120→        lines = raw.splitlines()
   121→
   122→        # Import-based unused checks.
   123→        if category in {"all", "imports"}:
   124→            for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
   125→                if line.lstrip() != line:
   126→                    continue
   127→                if not line.strip().startswith("import "):
   128→                    continue
   129→                for name in _extract_import_names(line):
   130→                    if name.startswith("_"):
   131→                        continue
   132→                    if _identifier_occurrences(code, name) <= 1:
   133→                        entries.append(
   134→                            {
   135→                                "file": filepath,
   136→                                "line": lineno,
   137→                                "col": max(1, line.find(name) + 1),
   138→                                "name": name,
   139→                                "category": "imports",
   140→                            }
   141→                        )
   142→
   143→        # Top-level variable/function/class declarations (non-exported only).
   144→        if category in {"all", "vars"}:
   145→            for lineno, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
   146→                if line.lstrip() != line:
   147→                    continue
   148→                m = _DECL_RE.match(line)
   149→                if not m:
   150→                    continue
   151→                exported, name = m.group(1), m.group(2)
   152→                if exported or name.startswith("_"):
   153→                    continue
   154→                if _identifier_occurrences(code, name) <= 1:
   155→                    entries.append(
   156→                        {
   157→                            "file": filepath,
   158→                            "line": lineno,
   159→                            "col": max(1, line.find(name) + 1),
   160→                            "name": name,
   161→                            "category": "vars",
   162→                        }
   163→                    )
   164→
   165→    return entries, len(files)
   166→
   167→
   168→def _contains_deno_markers(path: Path) -> bool:
   169→    """Return True when scan path is inside a Deno project boundary.
   170→
   171→    `deno.lock` alone is not sufficient in mixed repos (e.g., Supabase edge
   172→    functions + regular TypeScript app). It frequently exists at repo root and
   173→    would otherwise disable tsc-based unused detection globally.
   174→    """
   175→    markers = ("deno.json", "deno.jsonc", "import_map.json")
   176→    current = path.resolve()
   177→    root = get_project_root().resolve()
   178→    for parent in (current, *current.parents):
   179→        for marker in markers:
   180→            if (parent / marker).is_file():
   181→                return True
   182→        if parent == root:
   183→            break
   184→    return False
   185→
   186→
   187→def _has_deno_import_syntax(ts_files: list[str]) -> bool:
   188→    for filepath in ts_files:
   189→        full = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
   190→        content = read_file_text(str(full))
   191→        if content and _DENO_IMPORT_RE.search(content):
   192→            return True
   193→    return False
   194→
   195→
   196→def _should_use_deno_fallback(path: Path, ts_files: list[str]) -> bool:
   197→    normalized = path.resolve().as_posix().lower()
   198→    if normalized.endswith("/supabase/functions") or "/supabase/functions/" in normalized:
   199→        return True
   200→    if _contains_deno_markers(path):
   201→        return True
   202→    return _has_deno_import_syntax(ts_files)
   203→
   204→
   205→def detect_unused(path: Path, category: str = "all") -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   206→    ts_files = find_ts_files(path)
   207→    total_files = len(ts_files)
   208→    if _should_use_deno_fallback(path, ts_files):
   209→        return _detect_unused_fallback(path, category)
   210→
   211→    # Create a temporary tsconfig that enables declaration-read checks
   212→    # (the project tsconfig keeps declaration-read checks disabled)
   213→    tmp_tsconfig = {
   214→        "extends": "./tsconfig.app.json",
   215→        "compilerOptions": {
   216→            "noUnusedLocals": True,
   217→            "noUnusedParameters": True,
   218→        },
   219→    }
   220→    tmp_path = get_project_root() / "tsconfig.desloppify.json"
   221→    try:
   222→        safe_write_text(tmp_path, json.dumps(tmp_tsconfig, indent=2))
   223→        try:
   224→            result = subprocess.run(
   225→                ["npx", "tsc", "--project", str(tmp_path), "--noEmit"],
   226→                capture_output=True,
   227→                text=True,
   228→                cwd=get_project_root(),
   229→                timeout=120,
   230→                shell=(sys.platform == "win32"),
   231→            )
   232→        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as exc:
   233→            logger.debug("Falling back to source-based unused detection: %s", exc)
   234→            return _detect_unused_fallback(path, category)
   235→    finally:
   236→        tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   237→
   238→    entries = []
   239→    for line in result.stdout.splitlines() + result.stderr.splitlines():
   240→        m = TS6133_RE.match(line)
   241→        m2 = TS6192_RE.match(line) if not m else None
   242→        if not m and not m2:
   243→            continue
   244→        if m:
   245→            filepath, lineno, col, name = (
   246→                m.group(1),
   247→                int(m.group(2)),
   248→                int(m.group(3)),
   249→                m.group(4),
   250→            )
   251→            # Skip _ prefixed names (intentionally unused by convention)
   252→            if name.startswith("_"):
   253→                continue
   254→        else:
   255→            filepath, lineno, col = m2.group(1), int(m2.group(2)), int(m2.group(3))
   256→            name = "(entire import)"
   257→        # Scope to requested path
   258→        try:
   259→            full = Path(resolve_path(filepath))
   260→            if not str(full).startswith(str(path.resolve())):
   261→                continue
   262→        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
   263→            logger.debug("Skipping path scope check for %s: %s", filepath, exc)
   264→            continue
   265→        cat = _categorize_unused(filepath, lineno)
   266→        if category != "all" and cat != category:
   267→            continue
   268→        entries.append(
   269→            {
   270→                "file": filepath,
   271→                "line": lineno,
   272→                "col": col,
   273→                "name": name,
   274→                "category": cat,
   275→            }
   276→        )
   277→    return entries, total_files
   278→
   279→
   280→def _categorize_unused(filepath: str, lineno: int) -> str:
   281→    try:
   282→        p = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
   283→        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
   284→        if lineno <= len(lines):
   285→            src_line = lines[lineno - 1].strip()
   286→            if (
   287→                src_line.startswith("import ")
   288→                or "from '" in src_line
   289→                or 'from "' in src_line
   290→            ):
   291→                return "imports"
   292→            # If the line starts with a declaration keyword, it's definitely not an import
   293→            if src_line.startswith(
   294→                (
   295→                    "const ",
   296→                    "let ",
   297→                    "var ",
   298→                    "export ",
   299→                    "function ",
   300→                    "class ",
   301→                    "type ",
   302→                    "interface ",
   303→                )
   304→            ):
   305→                return "vars"
   306→            # Walk back to check if this line is within a multi-line import block
   307→            for back in range(1, 10):
   308→                idx = lineno - 1 - back
   309→                if idx < 0:
   310→                    break
   311→                prev = lines[idx].strip()
   312→                if prev.startswith("import "):
   313→                    return "imports"
   314→                # Stop at blank lines or non-import-continuation lines
   315→                if not prev or (
   316→                    not prev.startswith("{")
   317→                    and not prev.startswith(",")
   318→                    and "," not in prev
   319→                ):
   320→                    break
   321→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   322→        logger.debug("Unable to read %s for unused categorization: %s", filepath, exc)
   323→        return "imports"  # Safer fallback — import fixers are less destructive.
   324→    return "imports"
   325→
   326→
   327→def cmd_unused(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   328→    if _should_use_deno_fallback(Path(args.path), find_ts_files(Path(args.path))):
   329→        print(
   330→            colorize(
   331→                "Deno/edge TypeScript context detected — using source-based unused scan",
   332→                "dim",
   333→            ),
   334→            file=sys.stderr,
   335→        )
   336→    else:
   337→        print(colorize("Running tsc... (this may take a moment)", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   338→    entries, _ = detect_unused(Path(args.path), args.category)
   339→    if args.json:
   340→        print(json.dumps({"count": len(entries), "entries": entries}, indent=2))
   341→        return
   342→
   343→    if not entries:
   344→        print(colorize("No unused declarations found.", "green"))
   345→        return
   346→
   347→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   348→    for e in entries:
   349→        by_file[e["file"]].append(e)
   350→
   351→    by_cat: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   352→    for e in entries:
   353→        by_cat[e["category"]] += 1
   354→
   355→    print(
   356→        colorize(
   357→            f"\nUnused declarations: {len(entries)} across {len(by_file)} files\n",
   358→            "bold",
   359→        )
   360→    )
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("By category:", "cyan"))
   363→    for cat, count in sorted(by_cat.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
   364→        print(f"  {cat}: {count}")
   365→    print()
   366→
   367→    print(colorize("Top files:", "cyan"))
   368→    sorted_files = sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))
   369→    rows = []
   370→    for filepath, file_entries in sorted_files[: args.top]:
   371→        names = ", ".join(e["name"] for e in file_entries[:5])
   372→        if len(file_entries) > 5:
   373→            names += f", ... (+{len(file_entries) - 5})"
   374→        rows.append([rel(filepath), str(len(file_entries)), names])
   375→    print_table(["File", "Count", "Names"], rows, [55, 6, 50])
   376→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    changes: int = 0
    36→
    37→
    38→def _find_candidates(
    39→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    40→) -> list[str]:
    41→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    42→    candidates: list[str] = []
    43→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    44→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    45→            continue
    46→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    47→            candidates.append(fid)
    48→    return candidates
    49→
    50→
    51→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    53→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    54→    if issue is None:
    55→        return False
    56→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    57→
    58→
    59→def _supersede_id(
    60→    plan: PlanModel,
    61→    state: StateModel,
    62→    issue_id: str,
    63→    now: str,
    64→) -> bool:
    65→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    66→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    67→    detector = ""
    68→    file = ""
    69→    summary = ""
    70→    if issue:
    71→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    72→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    73→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    74→
    75→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    76→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    77→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    78→
    79→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    80→        "original_id": issue_id,
    81→        "original_detector": detector,
    82→        "original_file": file,
    83→        "original_summary": summary,
    84→        "status": "superseded",
    85→        "superseded_at": now,
    86→        "remapped_to": None,
    87→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    # Preserve any existing override note
    91→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    92→    if override_note:
    93→        entry["note"] = override_note
    94→
    95→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    96→
    97→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    98→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    99→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   100→    if issue_id in order:
   101→        order.remove(issue_id)
   102→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   103→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   104→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   105→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   106→        if issue_id in ids:
   107→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   110→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   111→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   112→        override["cluster"] = None
   113→        override["updated_at"] = now
   114→
   115→    return True
   116→
   117→
   118→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   119→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   120→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   121→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   122→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   123→
   124→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   125→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   126→        try:
   127→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   128→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   129→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   130→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   131→                to_prune.append(fid)
   132→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   133→            to_prune.append(fid)
   134→
   135→    for fid in to_prune:
   136→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   137→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   138→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   139→
   140→    return to_prune
   141→
   142→
   143→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   144→    plan: PlanModel,
   145→    state: StateModel,
   146→) -> ReconcileResult:
   147→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   148→
   149→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   150→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   151→    """
   152→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   153→    result = ReconcileResult()
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   156→
   157→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   158→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   159→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   161→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   162→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   163→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   164→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   165→
   166→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   167→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   168→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   169→    referenced_ids = {
   170→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   171→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    # Check each referenced ID
   175→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   176→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   177→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   178→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   179→                result.changes += 1
   180→
   181→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   182→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   183→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   184→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   185→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   186→            continue
   187→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   188→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   189→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   190→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        if not alive_ids:
   193→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   194→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   195→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   196→        result.changes += 1
   197→
   198→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   199→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   200→
   201→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   202→    if resurfaced:
   203→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   204→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   205→
   206→    # Prune old superseded entries
   207→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   208→    result.pruned = pruned
   209→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   210→
   211→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   212→    if result.changes > 0:
   213→        append_log_entry(
   214→            plan,
   215→            "reconcile",
   216→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   217→            actor="system",
   218→            detail={
   219→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   220→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   221→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   222→            },
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    return result
   226→
   227→
   228→@dataclass
   229→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   230→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   231→
   232→    new_ids: set[str]
   233→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   234→    triage_injected: bool
   235→
   236→
   237→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   238→    plan: PlanModel,
   239→    state: StateModel,
   240→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   241→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   242→
   243→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   244→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new issues to sync.
   245→    """
   246→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   247→    new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   248→    if not new_ids:
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   252→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   253→    existing = set(order)
   254→    added: list[str] = []
   255→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   256→        if fid not in existing:
   257→            order.append(fid)
   258→            added.append(fid)
   259→
   260→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   261→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   262→    triage_injected = bool(
   263→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   264→    )
   265→
   266→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   267→        new_ids=new_ids,
   268→        added_to_queue=added,
   269→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   270→    )
   271→
   272→
   273→__all__ = [
   274→    "ReconcileResult",
   275→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   276→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   277→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   278→]
   279→
```

1→"""AST detectors that operate on function/class nodes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import ast
