```
    11→
    12→
    13→def _is_test_file(filepath: str) -> bool:
    14→    """Return True when a path clearly points to a test module."""
    15→    normalized = filepath.replace("\\", "/")
    16→    return normalized.startswith("tests/") or "/tests/" in normalized
    17→
    18→
    19→def _detect_monster_functions(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
    20→    """Flag functions longer than 150 LOC."""
    21→    if not (hasattr(node, "end_lineno") and node.end_lineno):
    22→        return []
    23→    loc = node.end_lineno - node.lineno + 1
    24→    if loc > 150:
    25→        return [
    26→            {
    27→                "file": filepath,
    28→                "line": node.lineno,
    29→                "content": f"{node.name}() — {loc} LOC",
    30→            }
    31→        ]
    32→    return []
    33→
    34→
    35→def _detect_dead_functions(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
    36→    """Flag functions whose body is only pass, return, or return None."""
    37→    if node.decorator_list:
    38→        return []
    39→    body = node.body
    40→    if len(body) == 1:
    41→        stmt = body[0]
    42→        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass) or _is_return_none(stmt):
    43→            return [
    44→                {
    45→                    "file": filepath,
    46→                    "line": node.lineno,
    47→                    "content": f"{node.name}() — body is only {ast.dump(stmt)[:40]}",
    48→                }
    49→            ]
    50→    elif len(body) == 2:
    51→        first, second = body
    52→        if not _is_docstring(first):
    53→            return []
    54→        if isinstance(second, ast.Pass):
    55→            desc = "docstring + pass"
    56→        elif _is_return_none(second):
    57→            desc = "docstring + return None"
    58→        else:
    59→            return []
    60→        return [
    61→            {
    62→                "file": filepath,
    63→                "line": node.lineno,
    64→                "content": f"{node.name}() — {desc}",
    65→            }
    66→        ]
    67→    return []
    68→
    69→
    70→def _detect_deferred_imports(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Flag function-level imports (possible circular import workarounds)."""
    72→    if _is_test_file(filepath):
    73→        return []
    74→    _SKIP_MODULES = ("typing", "typing_extensions", "__future__")
    75→    for child in ast.walk(node):
    76→        if (
    77→            not isinstance(child, ast.Import | ast.ImportFrom)
    78→            or child.lineno <= node.lineno
    79→        ):
    80→            continue
    81→        module = getattr(child, "module", None) or ""
    82→        if module in _SKIP_MODULES:
    83→            continue
    84→        names = ", ".join(a.name for a in child.names[:3])
    85→        if len(child.names) > 3:
    86→            names += f", +{len(child.names) - 3}"
    87→        return [
    88→            {
    89→                "file": filepath,
    90→                "line": child.lineno,
    91→                "content": f"import {module or names} inside {node.name}()",
    92→            }
    93→        ]
    94→    return []
    95→
    96→
    97→def _detect_inline_classes(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
    98→    """Flag classes defined inside functions."""
    99→    results: list[dict] = []
   100→    for child in node.body:
   101→        if isinstance(child, ast.ClassDef):
   102→            results.append(
   103→                {
   104→                    "file": filepath,
   105→                    "line": child.lineno,
   106→                    "content": f"class {child.name} defined inside {node.name}()",
   107→                }
   108→            )
   109→    return results
   110→
   111→
   112→_FUNC_TYPES = (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.Lambda)
   113→_BLOCK_ATTRS = ("body", "handlers", "orelse", "finalbody")
   114→
   115→
   116→def _walk_inner_defs(
   117→    body: list[ast.AST], depth: int, inner_defs: list[ast.AST],
   118→) -> int:
   119→    """Recursively collect inner function/lambda defs and return max nesting depth."""
   120→    max_depth = 0
   121→    for child in body:
   122→        if isinstance(child, _FUNC_TYPES):
   123→            inner_defs.append(child)
   124→            current_depth = depth + 1
   125→            max_depth = max(max_depth, current_depth)
   126→            child_body = getattr(child, "body", None)
   127→            if isinstance(child_body, list):
   128→                max_depth = max(
   129→                    max_depth,
   130→                    _walk_inner_defs(child_body, current_depth, inner_defs),
   131→                )
   132→            elif isinstance(child, ast.Lambda):
   133→                max_depth = max(
   134→                    max_depth,
   135→                    _collect_nested_lambdas(child, current_depth, inner_defs),
   136→                )
   137→        else:
   138→            for attr in _BLOCK_ATTRS:
   139→                sub_body = getattr(child, attr, None)
   140→                if isinstance(sub_body, list):
   141→                    max_depth = max(
   142→                        max_depth, _walk_inner_defs(sub_body, depth, inner_defs),
   143→                    )
   144→    return max_depth
   145→
   146→
   147→def _collect_nested_lambdas(
   148→    parent: ast.Lambda, depth: int, inner_defs: list[ast.AST],
   149→) -> int:
   150→    """Walk a lambda's expression body for nested lambdas."""
   151→    max_depth = depth
   152→    for sub in ast.walk(parent.body):
   153→        if isinstance(sub, ast.Lambda) and sub is not parent:
   154→            inner_defs.append(sub)
   155→            max_depth = max(max_depth, depth + 1)
   156→    return max_depth
   157→
   158→
   159→def _format_inner_def_names(inner_defs: list[ast.AST]) -> str:
   160→    """Format inner def names for the issue content string."""
   161→    names = [
   162→        getattr(d, "name", "<lambda>")
   163→        for d in inner_defs[:5]
   164→        if isinstance(d, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef)
   165→    ]
   166→    names_str = ", ".join(names) if names else "<lambdas>"
   167→    if len(inner_defs) > 5:
   168→        names_str += ", ..."
   169→    return names_str
   170→
   171→
   172→def _detect_nested_closures(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Flag functions with deeply nested inner defs or too many inner defs total."""
   174→    inner_defs: list[ast.AST] = []
   175→    max_depth = _walk_inner_defs(node.body, 0, inner_defs)
   176→
   177→    if max_depth < 2 and len(inner_defs) < 3:
   178→        return []
   179→    return [
   180→        {
   181→            "file": filepath,
   182→            "line": node.lineno,
   183→            "content": (
   184→                f"{node.name}() — {len(inner_defs)} inner defs"
   185→                f" (depth {max_depth}): {_format_inner_def_names(inner_defs)}"
   186→            ),
   187→        }
   188→    ]
   189→
   190→
   191→def _collect_single_list_assignments(body: list[ast.AST]) -> dict[str, int]:
   192→    """Return {name: lineno} for `x = [single_value]` assignments."""
   193→    result: dict[str, int] = {}
   194→    for stmt in body:
   195→        if not isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign) or len(stmt.targets) != 1:
   196→            continue
   197→        target = stmt.targets[0]
   198→        if (
   199→            isinstance(target, ast.Name)
   200→            and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.List)
   201→            and len(stmt.value.elts) == 1
   202→        ):
   203→            result[target.id] = stmt.lineno
   204→    return result
   205→
   206→
   207→def _find_subscript_zero_refs(
   208→    node: ast.AST, candidate_names: set[str],
   209→) -> set[str]:
   210→    """Find names from candidate_names accessed as x[0] inside nested functions."""
   211→    used: set[str] = set()
   212→    for child in ast.walk(node):
   213→        if child is node or not isinstance(child, _FUNC_TYPES):
   214→            continue
   215→        for sub in ast.walk(child):
   216→            if not isinstance(sub, ast.Subscript):
   217→                continue
   218→            if not isinstance(sub.value, ast.Name):
   219→                continue
   220→            if sub.value.id not in candidate_names:
   221→                continue
   222→            if isinstance(sub.slice, ast.Constant) and sub.slice.value == 0:
   223→                used.add(sub.value.id)
   224→    return used
   225→
   226→
   227→def _detect_mutable_ref_hack(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
   228→    """Flag mutable-list-as-ref hacks: x = [value] then x[0] inside a nested function."""
   229→    single_list_names = _collect_single_list_assignments(node.body)
   230→    if not single_list_names:
   231→        return []
   232→
   233→    used_names = _find_subscript_zero_refs(node, set(single_list_names))
   234→    return [
   235→        {
   236→            "file": filepath,
   237→            "line": single_list_names[name],
   238→            "content": (
   239→                f"{name} = [v] in {node.name}()"
   240→                " — mutable-list ref hack (use nonlocal or a dataclass)"
   241→            ),
   242→        }
   243→        for name in sorted(used_names)
   244→    ]
   245→
   246→
   247→# Node types that contribute to cyclomatic complexity.
   248→_DECISION_TYPES = (
   249→    ast.If, ast.IfExp, ast.For, ast.AsyncFor, ast.While,
   250→    ast.ExceptHandler, ast.With, ast.AsyncWith, ast.Assert,
   251→)
   252→
   253→
   254→def _compute_cyclomatic_complexity(node: ast.AST) -> int:
   255→    """Compute cyclomatic complexity for a function AST node."""
   256→    complexity = 1
   257→    for child in ast.walk(node):
   258→        if isinstance(child, _DECISION_TYPES):
   259→            complexity += 1
   260→        elif isinstance(child, ast.BoolOp):
   261→            complexity += len(child.values) - 1
   262→    return complexity
   263→
   264→
   265→def _detect_high_cyclomatic_complexity(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
   266→    """Flag functions with cyclomatic complexity > 12."""
   267→    complexity = _compute_cyclomatic_complexity(node)
   268→    if complexity > 12:
   269→        return [
   270→            {
   271→                "file": filepath,
   272→                "line": node.lineno,
   273→                "content": f"{node.name}() — cyclomatic complexity {complexity}",
   274→            }
   275→        ]
   276→    return []
   277→
   278→
   279→def _detect_lru_cache_mutable(
   280→    filepath: str,
   281→    node: ast.AST,
   282→    tree: ast.Module,
   283→) -> list[dict]:
   284→    """Flag @lru_cache/@cache functions that reference module-level mutable variables.
   285→
   286→    Finds globals referenced in the function body that aren't in the parameter list,
   287→    checking if those names are assigned to mutable values at module level.
   288→    """
   289→    # Check if this function has @lru_cache or @cache decorator
   290→    has_cache = False
   291→    for dec in node.decorator_list:
   292→        if isinstance(dec, ast.Name) and dec.id in ("lru_cache", "cache"):
   293→            has_cache = True
   294→        elif isinstance(dec, ast.Call) and isinstance(dec.func, ast.Name):
   295→            if dec.func.id in ("lru_cache", "cache"):
   296→                has_cache = True
   297→        elif isinstance(dec, ast.Attribute) and dec.attr in ("lru_cache", "cache"):
   298→            has_cache = True
   299→    if not has_cache:
   300→        return []
   301→
   302→    # Get parameter names
   303→    param_names = set()
   304→    for arg in node.args.args + node.args.posonlyargs + node.args.kwonlyargs:
   305→        param_names.add(arg.arg)
   306→    if node.args.vararg:
   307→        param_names.add(node.args.vararg.arg)
   308→    if node.args.kwarg:
   309→        param_names.add(node.args.kwarg.arg)
   310→
   311→    # Collect module-level mutable assignments
   312→    module_mutables = set()
   313→    for stmt in tree.body:
   314→        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign):
   315→            for target in stmt.targets:
   316→                if isinstance(target, ast.Name) and isinstance(
   317→                    stmt.value, ast.List | ast.Dict | ast.Set | ast.Call
   318→                ):
   319→                    module_mutables.add(target.id)
   320→        elif (
   321→            isinstance(stmt, ast.AnnAssign)
   322→            and stmt.target
   323→            and isinstance(stmt.target, ast.Name)
   324→        ):
   325→            if stmt.value and isinstance(
   326→                stmt.value, ast.List | ast.Dict | ast.Set | ast.Call
   327→            ):
   328→                module_mutables.add(stmt.target.id)
   329→
   330→    # Find Name references in function body that point to module-level mutables
   331→    for child in ast.walk(node):
   332→        if (
   333→            isinstance(child, ast.Name)
   334→            and child.id in module_mutables
   335→            and child.id not in param_names
   336→        ):
   337→            return [
   338→                {
   339→                    "file": filepath,
   340→                    "line": node.lineno,
   341→                    "content": f"@lru_cache on {node.name}() reads mutable global '{child.id}'",
   342→                }
   343→            ]
   344→    return []
   345→
```

```
    24→def _render_workflow_stage(item: dict) -> None:

    87→def _render_issue_detail(item: dict, *, single_item: bool = False) -> dict:

    98→        if single_item:

   119→    if detail.get("lines"):
   120→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   121→    if detail.get("category"):
   122→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   123→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   124→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   125→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   126→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   127→
   128→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   129→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   130→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   131→        if snippet:
   132→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   133→            print(snippet)
   134→
   135→    return detail
   136→
   137→
   138→def _render_dimension_context(detector: str, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   139→    if not dim_scores:
   140→        return
   141→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   142→    if not dimension or dimension.name not in dim_scores:
   143→        return
   144→    dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   145→    strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   149→            f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   150→            f"({dimension_score.get('failing', 0)} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   151→            "dim",
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _render_detector_impact_estimate(
   157→    detector: str, dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict,
   158→) -> None:
   159→    try:
   160→        impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   161→        if impact > 0:
   162→            print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   163→            return
   164→
   165→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   166→        if not dimension or dimension.name not in dim_scores:
   167→            return
   168→        issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("failing", 0)
   169→        if issues <= 1:
   170→            return
   171→        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   172→        if bulk > 0:
   173→            print(colorize(
   174→                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   175→                "cyan",
   176→            ))
   177→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   178→        log(f"  score impact estimate skipped: {exc}")
   179→
   180→
   181→def _render_review_dimension_drag(item: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   182→    try:
   183→        dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   184→        if not dim_key:
   185→            return
   186→        breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   187→        [REDACTED](dim_key)
   188→        for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   189→            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   190→                continue
   191→            if _normalized_dimension_key(entry.get("name", "")) != target_key:
   192→                continue
   193→            drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   194→            if drag > 0.01:
   195→                print(colorize(
   196→                    f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   197→                    "cyan",
   198→                ))
   199→            return
   200→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   201→        log(f"  dimension drag estimate skipped: {exc}")
   202→
   203→
   204→def _render_score_impact(
   205→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict | None,
   206→) -> None:
   207→    """Render dimension score context and impact estimates."""
   208→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   209→    _render_dimension_context(detector, dim_scores)
   210→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   211→        _render_detector_impact_estimate(detector, dim_scores, potentials)
   212→        return
   213→    if detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   214→        _render_review_dimension_drag(item, dim_scores)
   215→
   216→
   217→_KIND_RENDERERS = {
   218→    "cluster": _render_cluster_item,
   219→    "workflow_stage": _render_workflow_stage,
   220→    "workflow_action": _render_workflow_action,
   221→}
   222→
   223→
   224→def _render_item_type(item: dict) -> None:
   225→    detector = item.get("detector")
   226→    if detector == "review":
   227→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   228→        return
   229→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   230→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   231→
   232→
   233→def _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item: dict, issues_scoped: dict) -> None:
   234→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   235→    if not is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   236→        return
   237→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   238→    similar_count = sum(
   239→        1
   240→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   241→        if issue.get("detector") == detector_name and issue["status"] == "open"
   242→    )
   243→    if similar_count <= 1:
   244→        return
   245→    print(
   246→        colorize(
   247→            f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar issues. "
   248→            f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   249→            "cyan",
   250→        )
   251→    )
   252→
   253→
   254→def _render_item_explain(
   255→    item: dict, detail: dict, confidence: str, dim_scores: dict,
   256→) -> None:
   257→    explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   258→    count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   259→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   260→    base = (
   261→        f"ranked by confidence={confidence}, "
   262→        f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   263→    )
   264→    if dim_scores and detector:
   265→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   266→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   267→            ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   268→            base += (
   269→                f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   270→                f"({ds.get('failing', 0)} open issues)"
   271→            )
   272→    if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   273→        [REDACTED](item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", ""))
   274→        if dim_key:
   275→            for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   276→                if _normalized_dimension_key(ds_name) != dim_key:
   277→                    continue
   278→                score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   279→                if isinstance(score_val, int | float):
   280→                    score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}"
   281→                else:
   282→                    score_str = str(score_val)
   283→                base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   284→                break
   285→    policy = explanation.get("policy")
   286→    if policy:
   287→        base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   288→    print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   289→
   290→
   291→def _render_item(
   292→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   293→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   294→    single_item: bool = False,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    kind = item.get("kind")
   297→    kind_renderer = _KIND_RENDERERS.get(kind)
   298→    if kind_renderer is not None:
   299→        kind_renderer(item)
   300→        return
   301→
   302→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   303→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   304→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   305→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   306→    _render_item_type(item)
   307→
   308→    if item.get("kind", "issue") == "subjective_dimension":
   309→        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)
   310→        return
   311→
   312→    detail = _render_issue_detail(item, single_item=single_item)
   313→    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)
   314→    _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item, issues_scoped)
   315→    if explain:
   316→        _render_item_explain(item, detail, confidence, dim_scores)
   317→
   318→
   319→def _item_label(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> str:
   320→    queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   321→    if queue_pos and total > 1:
   322→        return f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   323→    if total > 1:
   324→        return f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}]"
   325→    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   326→    return f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   327→
   328→
   329→def render_terminal_items(
   330→    items: list[dict],
   331→    dim_scores: dict,
   332→    issues_scoped: dict,
   333→    *,
   334→    group: str,
   335→    explain: bool,
   336→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   337→    plan: dict | None = None,
   338→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   339→) -> None:
   340→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   341→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   342→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   343→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   344→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   345→        total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   346→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   347→
   348→    if group != "item":
   349→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   350→        return
   351→
   352→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   353→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   354→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   355→    )
   356→
   357→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   358→        if idx > 0:
   359→            print()
   360→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   361→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   362→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   363→            continue
   364→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   365→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   366→        _render_item(
   367→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   368→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   369→        )
   370→
   371→
   372→__all__ = [
   373→    "render_terminal_items",
   374→]
   375→
```

```
    28→def scorecard_subjective(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    dim_scores: dict,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    36→        state,
    37→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    38→    )

    40→
    41→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    42→    issues_scoped: dict,

    45→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(issues_scoped)

    47→
    48→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    49→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    50→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    51→
    52→
    53→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    54→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    55→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    56→        return "[review]"
    57→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    58→        return "[auto]"
    59→    return ""
    60→
    61→
    62→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    63→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    64→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    65→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    66→        for item in grouped_items:
    67→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    68→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    69→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    70→            print(
    71→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    72→            )
    73→
    74→

    81→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:

   102→

   104→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   105→    print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   106→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))

   119→            "dim",

   121→    )

   123→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   124→
   125→
   126→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   127→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   128→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   129→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   130→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   131→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   132→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   133→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   134→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   135→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   136→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   137→
   138→    members = item.get("members", [])
   139→    if members:
   140→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   141→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   142→
   143→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   144→    if primary_command:
   145→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   146→
   147→    if is_optional:
   148→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   152→
   153→
   154→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   155→    del explain
   156→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   157→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   158→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   159→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   160→    if (
   161→        total == 1
   162→        and len(items) == 1
   163→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   164→    ):
   165→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   166→    else:
   167→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   168→
   169→
   170→def show_empty_queue(
   171→    queue: dict,
   172→    strict: float | None,
   173→    *,
   174→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   175→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   176→) -> bool:
   177→    del target_strict
   178→    if queue.get("items"):
   179→        return False
   180→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   181→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   182→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   183→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   184→        print(colorize(
   185→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   186→            "cyan",
   187→        ))
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        return True
   193→
   194→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   195→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   196→    return True
   197→
   198→
   199→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   200→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   201→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   202→    tag = effort_tag(item)

   204→    fid = item.get("id", "")

   206→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   207→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   208→
```

```
   242→
   243→
   244→def _script_is_documented(readme_text: str, script_name: str) -> bool:
   245→    escaped = re.escape(script_name)
   246→    command_patterns = [
   247→        rf"\bnpm\s+(?:run\s+)?{escaped}\b",
   248→        rf"\bpnpm\s+{escaped}\b",
   249→        rf"\byarn\s+{escaped}\b",
   250→        rf"\bbun\s+run\s+{escaped}\b",
   251→    ]
   252→    if any(re.search(pattern, readme_text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) for pattern in command_patterns):
   253→        return True
   254→    return bool(re.search(rf"`{escaped}`", readme_text))
   255→
   256→
   257→def _detect_non_ts_asset_smells(path: Path, smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> int:
   258→    """Scan adjacent non-TS assets (CSS/docs) for common repo-health smells."""
   259→    scanned_files = 0
   260→    css_files = find_source_files(path, [".css", ".scss", ".sass", ".less"])
   261→    scanned_files += len(css_files)
   262→
   263→    for filepath in css_files:
   264→        try:
   265→            full = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else get_project_root() / filepath
   266→            content = full.read_text()
   267→            lines = content.splitlines()
   268→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   269→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"read stylesheet smell candidate {filepath}", exc)
   270→            continue
   271→
   272→        if len(lines) >= 300:
   273→            smell_counts["css_monolith"].append(
   274→                {
   275→                    "file": filepath,
   276→                    "line": 1,
   277→                    "content": f"{len(lines)} LOC stylesheet",
   278→                }
   279→            )
   280→
   281→        important_count = content.count("!important")
   282→        if important_count >= 8:
   283→            first_line = next(
   284→                (idx + 1 for idx, line in enumerate(lines) if "!important" in line),
   285→                1,
   286→            )
   287→            smell_counts["css_important_overuse"].append(
   288→                {
   289→                    "file": filepath,
   290→                    "line": first_line,
   291→                    "content": f"{important_count} !important declarations",
   292→                }
   293→            )
   294→
   295→    readme_path = get_project_root() / "README.md"
   296→    package_path = get_project_root() / "package.json"
   297→    if not readme_path.is_file() or not package_path.is_file():
   298→        return scanned_files
   299→
   300→    scanned_files += 1
   301→    try:
   302→        readme_text = readme_path.read_text()
   303→        package_payload = json.loads(package_path.read_text())
   304→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   305→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "read package/readme for docs drift smell", exc)
   306→        return scanned_files
   307→
   308→    scripts = package_payload.get("scripts")
   309→    if not isinstance(scripts, dict):
   310→        return scanned_files
   311→
   312→    key_scripts = [
   313→        script for script in ("dev", "build", "test", "lint", "typecheck") if script in scripts
   314→    ]
   315→    if len(key_scripts) < 2:
   316→        return scanned_files
   317→
   318→    missing = [script for script in key_scripts if not _script_is_documented(readme_text, script)]
   319→    if len(missing) >= 2:
   320→        smell_counts["docs_scripts_drift"].append(
   321→            {
   322→                "file": "README.md",
   323→                "line": 1,
   324→                "content": f"Missing script docs: {', '.join(missing[:5])}",
   325→            }
   326→        )
   327→    return scanned_files
   328→
   329→
   330→def detect_smells(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   331→    """Detect TypeScript/React code smell patterns across the codebase.
   332→
   333→    Returns (entries, total_files_checked).
   334→    """
   335→    checks = TS_SMELL_CHECKS
   336→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {s["id"]: [] for s in checks}
   337→    files = find_ts_files(path)
   338→
   339→    for filepath in files:
   340→        if "node_modules" in filepath or ".d.ts" in filepath:
   341→            continue
   342→        try:
   343→            p = (
   344→                Path(filepath)
   345→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   346→                else get_project_root() / filepath
   347→            )
   348→            content = p.read_text()
   349→            lines = content.splitlines()
   350→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   351→            log_best_effort_failure(
   352→                logger, f"read TypeScript smell candidate {filepath}", exc
   353→            )
   354→            continue
   355→
   356→        # Build line state for string/comment filtering
   357→        line_state = _build_ts_line_state(lines)
   358→        ctx = _FileContext(filepath, content, lines, line_state)
   359→
   360→        # Regex-based smells
   361→        for check in checks:
   362→            if check["pattern"] is None:
   363→                continue
   364→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   365→                # Skip lines inside block comments or template literals
   366→                if i in line_state:
   367→                    continue
   368→                m = re.search(check["pattern"], line)
   369→                if not m:
   370→                    continue
   371→                # Check if match is inside a single-line string or comment
   372→                if _ts_match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   373→                    continue
   374→                # Skip URLs assigned to module-level constants
   375→                if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   376→                    r"^(?:export\s+)?(?:const|let|var)\s+[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=",
   377→                    line.strip(),
   378→                ):
   379→                    continue
   380→                smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   381→                    {
   382→                        "file": filepath,
   383→                        "line": i + 1,
   384→                        "content": line.strip()[:100],
   385→                    }
   386→                )
   387→
   388→        # Multi-line smell detectors (brace-tracked, uniform ctx signature)
   389→        for detector in _MULTI_LINE_DETECTORS:
   390→            detector(ctx, smell_counts)
   391→
   392→    non_ts_files = _detect_non_ts_asset_smells(path, smell_counts)
   393→
   394→    # Build summary entries sorted by severity then count
   395→    severity_order = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
   396→    entries = []
   397→    for check in checks:
   398→        matches = smell_counts[check["id"]]
   399→        if matches:
   400→            entries.append(
   401→                {
   402→                    "id": check["id"],
   403→                    "label": check["label"],
   404→                    "severity": check["severity"],
   405→                    "count": len(matches),
   406→                    "files": len(set(m["file"] for m in matches)),
   407→                    "matches": matches[:50],
   408→                }
   409→            )
   410→    entries.sort(key=lambda e: (severity_order.get(e["severity"], 9), -e["count"]))
   411→    return entries, len(files) + non_ts_files
   412→
```

```
    46→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    47→class NextOptions:

    49→

    60→    @classmethod
    61→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> NextOptions:
    62→        """Build from an argparse Namespace, applying defaults for missing attrs."""
    63→        return cls(

    76→def _scorecard_subjective(
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→) -> list[dict]:
    80→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    82→
    83→
    84→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    85→    state: dict,
    86→    dim_scores: dict,
    87→    *,
    88→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    89→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    90→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    91→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    92→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    93→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    96→        if strict_val < threshold:
    97→            low.append(
    98→                (
    99→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
   100→                    strict_val,
   101→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   102→                )
   103→            )
   104→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   105→    return low

   108→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
   110→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   111→    state = runtime.state
   112→    config = runtime.config
   113→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   117→    if skill_warning:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   119→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   120→    if config_warning:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   122→
   123→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   124→    _build_and_render_queue(args, state, config)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
   128→    plan_data: dict | None,
   129→    *,
   130→    cluster_arg: str | None,
   131→    scope: str | None,
   132→) -> str | None:
   133→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   134→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   135→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   136→        if active_cluster:
   137→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   138→    return effective_cluster
   139→

   141→def _build_next_payload(
   142→    *,
   143→    queue: dict,
   144→    items: list[dict],
   145→    state: dict,
   146→    narrative: dict,
   147→    plan_data: dict | None,
   148→) -> dict:
   149→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   150→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   151→    )
   152→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   153→    payload["overall_score"] = scores.overall
   154→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   155→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict
   156→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   157→        state,
   158→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   159→    )
   160→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   161→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   162→    ]
   163→    return payload
   164→

   166→def _emit_requested_output(
   167→    opts: NextOptions,

   187→def _plan_queue_context(

   201→
   202→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   203→    try:
   204→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   205→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   206→        return raw_potentials or None
   207→
   208→
   209→def _build_and_render_queue(args: argparse.Namespace, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   210→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   211→
   212→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   213→
   214→    # Load the living plan
   215→    plan = load_plan()
   216→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   217→    if (
   218→        plan.get("queue_order")
   219→        or plan.get("overrides")
   220→        or plan.get("clusters")
   221→    ):
   222→        plan_data = plan
   223→
   224→    # Build unified context once — all downstream consumers agree on
   225→    # plan, target_strict, and subjective visibility policy.
   226→    ctx = queue_context(
   227→        state, config=config, plan=plan_data, target_strict=target_strict,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   231→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   232→        plan_data,
   233→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   234→        scope=opts.scope,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→    queue = build_work_queue(
   238→        state,
   239→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   240→            count=opts.count,
   241→            scope=opts.scope,
   242→            status=opts.status,
   243→            include_subjective=True,
   244→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   245→            explain=opts.explain,
   246→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   247→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   248→            context=ctx,
   249→        ),
   250→    )
   251→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   252→
   253→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   254→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   255→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   256→        state,
   257→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   258→    )
   259→
   260→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   261→        queue=queue,
   262→        items=items,
   263→        state=state,
   264→        narrative=narrative,
   265→        plan_data=plan_data,
   266→    )
   267→    write_query(payload)
   268→
   269→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items):
   270→        return
   271→
   272→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   273→    issues_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_issues(
   274→        state.get("issues", {}),
   275→        state.get("scan_path"),
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   279→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   280→        state=state,
   281→        plan_data=plan_data,
   282→        context=ctx,
   283→    )
   284→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   285→
   286→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   287→    strict_score = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   288→    if _show_empty_queue(
   289→        queue,
   290→        strict_score,
   291→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   292→        target_strict=target_strict,
   293→    ):
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   297→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   298→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   299→        items, dim_scores, issues_scoped, group=opts.group, explain=opts.explain,
   300→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   301→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   302→    )
   303→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   304→    next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   305→    next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   306→        state,
   307→        dim_scores,
   308→        issues_scoped,
   309→        strict_score=strict_score,
   310→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   311→        queue_total=queue_total,
   312→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   313→        breakdown=breakdown,
   314→    )
   315→    print()
   316→
   317→    if items and plan_data:
   318→        print_user_message(
   319→            "Hey — the task details are printed above. Just start"
   320→            " working on it. When you're done, resolve it with"
   321→            " `desloppify plan resolve` and then run `desloppify"
   322→            " next` again. If this doesn't seem like the right"
   323→            " priority, run `desloppify plan show` to see the full"
   324→            " queue, or `desloppify plan --help` for all plan tools."
   325→            " Don't stop until you're done — unless I've told you"
   326→            " otherwise, just keep going."
   327→        )
   328→
   329→
   330→__all__ = ["NextOptions", "_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   331→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),
    56→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    57→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    58→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    59→        "file": item.get("file"),
    60→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state
    67→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:
    74→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    75→
    76→    # Plan metadata
    77→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    81→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    82→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    84→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    86→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    87→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(
    95→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    96→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   101→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   102→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   103→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   104→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   105→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   109→        "command": command,
   110→        "items": serialized,
   111→        "queue": queue_section,
   112→        "narrative": narrative,
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    if plan and (
   116→        plan.get("queue_order")
   117→        or plan.get("skipped")
   118→        or plan.get("clusters")
   119→    ):
   120→        clusters_summary = []
   121→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   122→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   123→            clusters_summary.append({
   124→                "name": name,
   125→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   126→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   127→            })
   128→        payload["plan"] = {
   129→            "active": True,
   130→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   131→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   132→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   133→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   134→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   135→        }
   136→
   137→    return payload
   138→
   139→
   140→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   141→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   142→    lines = [
   143→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   144→        "",
   145→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   146→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   147→    ]
   148→    for item in items:
   149→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   150→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   151→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   152→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   153→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   154→    lines.append("")
   155→    return "\n".join(lines)
   156→
   157→
   158→def write_output_file(
   159→    output_file: str,
   160→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   161→    item_count: int,
   162→    *,
   163→    safe_write_text_fn,
   164→    colorize_fn,
   165→) -> bool:
   166→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   167→    try:
   168→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   169→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   170→    except OSError as exc:
   171→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   172→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   173→        return False
   174→    return True
   175→
   176→
   177→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   178→    output_format: str,
   179→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   180→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   181→) -> bool:
   182→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   183→    renderers = {
   184→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   185→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   186→    }
   187→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   188→    if renderer is None:
   189→        return False
   190→    renderer()
   191→    return True
   192→
   193→
   194→__all__ = [
   195→    "build_query_payload",
   196→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   197→    "render_markdown",
   198→    "serialize_item",
   199→    "write_output_file",
   200→]
   201→
```

```
1→"""Post-render nudges and resolution hints for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     6→    QueueBreakdown,
     7→    format_plan_delta,
     8→    format_queue_block,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.reporting.subjective import (
    11→    build_subjective_followup,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, log
    15→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import compute_health_breakdown
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    17→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    18→    is_holistic_subjective_issue,
    19→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    20→)
    21→
    22→from .render_support import (
    23→    is_auto_fix_command,
    24→    scorecard_subjective,
    25→    subjective_coverage_breakdown,
    26→)
    27→
    28→
    29→def render_uncommitted_reminder(plan: dict | None) -> None:
    30→    """Show a subtle reminder if there are uncommitted resolved issues."""
    31→    if plan is None:
    32→        return
    33→    try:
    34→        config = load_config()
    35→        if not config.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
    36→            return
    37→
    38→        uncommitted = plan.get("uncommitted_issues", [])
    39→        if not uncommitted:
    40→            return
    41→
    42→        count = len(uncommitted)
    43→        print(colorize(
    44→            f"\n  {count} resolved issue{'s' if count != 1 else ''} uncommitted"
    45→            " — `desloppify plan commit-log` to review",
    46→            "dim",
    47→        ))
    48→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
    49→        log(f"  uncommitted reminder skipped: {exc}")
    50→
    51→
    52→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
    53→    if len(items) != 1:
    54→        return
    55→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "issue")
    56→    if kind in ("cluster", "workflow_stage", "workflow_action"):
    57→        return  # These kinds have their own resolution hints
    58→    if kind != "issue":
    59→        return
    60→    item = items[0]
    61→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
    62→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
    63→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
    64→        primary = item.get(
    65→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
    66→        )
    67→        print(f"    {primary}")
    68→        if is_holistic_subjective_issue(item):
    69→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
    73→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
    74→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
    75→        print(f"    {primary}")
    76→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
    77→    else:
    78→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
    79→
    80→    print(
    81→        f'    desloppify plan resolve "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
    82→    )
    83→    print(
    84→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
    85→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _render_frozen_queue_status(
    90→    *,
    91→    strict_score: float | None,
    92→    queue_total: int,
    93→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
    94→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
    95→) -> bool:
    96→    if queue_total <= 0 or plan_start_strict is None:
    97→        return False
    98→    if breakdown is not None:
    99→        block = format_queue_block(
   100→            breakdown, frozen_score=plan_start_strict, live_score=strict_score
   101→        )
   102→        print()
   103→        for text, style in block:
   104→            print(colorize(text, style))
   105→        print(colorize(
   106→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   107→            "dim",
   108→        ))
   109→        return True
   110→
   111→    delta_str = ""
   112→    if strict_score is not None:
   113→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(strict_score, plan_start_strict)
   114→    if delta_str:
   115→        score_line = (
   116→            f"\n  Score: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 "
   117→            f"(plan start: {plan_start_strict:.1f}, {delta_str})"
   118→        )
   119→    else:
   120→        score_line = f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {plan_start_strict:.1f}/100"
   121→    print(colorize(score_line, "cyan"))
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            f"  Queue: {queue_total} item{'s' if queue_total != 1 else ''}"
   125→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   126→            "dim",
   127→        )
   128→    )
   129→    return True
   130→
   131→
   132→def _render_north_star(
   133→    *,
   134→    strict_score: float | None,
   135→    target_strict_score: float,
   136→) -> None:
   137→    if strict_score is None:
   138→        return
   139→    gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   140→    if gap > 0:
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   144→                "cyan",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→        return
   148→    print(
   149→        colorize(
   150→            f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   151→            "green",
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _render_live_queue_block(
   157→    *,
   158→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
   159→    queue_total: int,
   160→    plan_start_strict: float | None,
   161→) -> None:
   162→    if breakdown is None or queue_total <= 0 or plan_start_strict is not None:
   163→        return
   164→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   165→    for text, style in block:
   166→        print(colorize(text, style))
   167→
   168→
   169→def _objective_remaining(
   170→    queue_total: int,
   171→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None,
   172→) -> int:
   173→    if breakdown is None:
   174→        return max(0, queue_total)
   175→    return max(0, breakdown.queue_total - breakdown.subjective)
   176→
   177→
   178→def _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   179→    try:
   180→        health_breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   181→        subjective_drag = sum(
   182→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   183→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   184→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") == "subjective"
   185→        )
   186→        mechanical_drag = sum(
   187→            float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   188→            for e in health_breakdown.get("entries", [])
   189→            if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("component") != "subjective"
   190→        )
   191→        if subjective_drag <= mechanical_drag or subjective_drag <= 5.0:
   192→            return
   193→        print(colorize(
   194→            f"\n  Subjective dimensions are the main bottleneck "
   195→            f"(-{subjective_drag:.0f} pts vs -{mechanical_drag:.0f} pts mechanical).",
   196→            "yellow",
   197→        ))
   198→        print(colorize(
   199→            "  Code fixes alone won't close the gap — run "
   200→            "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   201→            "to re-score.",
   202→            "yellow",
   203→        ))
   204→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   205→        log(f"  subjective bottleneck banner skipped: {exc}")
   206→
   207→
   208→def _subjective_summary_parts(
   209→    *,
   210→    followup,
   211→    unassessed_subjective: list[str],
   212→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   213→    issues_scoped: dict,
   214→    coverage_open: int,
   215→) -> list[str]:
   216→    parts: list[str] = []
   217→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   218→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   219→    stale_count = sum(1 for entry in subjective_entries if entry.get("stale"))
   220→    open_review_count = sum(
   221→        1
   222→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   223→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") == "review"
   224→    )
   225→    if low_dims:
   226→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   227→    if stale_count:
   228→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   229→    if unassessed_count:
   230→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   231→    if open_review_count:
   232→        parts.append(
   233→            f"{open_review_count} review issue{'s' if open_review_count != 1 else ''} open"
   234→        )
   235→    if coverage_open > 0:
   236→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   237→    return parts
   238→
   239→
   240→def render_followup_nudges(
   241→    state: dict,
   242→    dim_scores: dict,
   243→    issues_scoped: dict,
   244→    *,
   245→    strict_score: float | None,
   246→    target_strict_score: float,
   247→    queue_total: int = 0,
   248→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   249→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   250→) -> None:
   251→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   252→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   253→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   254→        state,
   255→        subjective_entries,
   256→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   257→        max_quality_items=3,
   258→        max_integrity_items=5,
   259→    )
   260→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   261→    rendered_frozen = _render_frozen_queue_status(
   262→        strict_score=strict_score,
   263→        queue_total=queue_total,
   264→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   265→        breakdown=breakdown,
   266→    )
   267→    if not rendered_frozen:
   268→        _render_north_star(
   269→            strict_score=strict_score,
   270→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   271→        )
   272→    _render_live_queue_block(
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→        queue_total=queue_total,
   275→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    # Subjective bottleneck banner — only shown when the objective queue is
   279→    # clear.  While objective items remain, the queue is the single authority
   280→    # on what to work on next; no need to distract with subjective advice.
   281→    objective_remaining = _objective_remaining(queue_total, breakdown)
   282→    if strict_score is not None and dim_scores and objective_remaining <= 0:
   283→        _render_subjective_bottleneck(dim_scores)
   284→
   285→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   286→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   287→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   288→
   289→    # Rescan nudge after structural work
   290→    if queue_total > 10:
   291→        print(colorize(
   292→            "\n  Tip: after structural fixes (splitting files, moving code), rescan to "
   293→            "let cascade effects settle: `desloppify scan --path .`",
   294→            "dim",
   295→        ))
   296→
   297→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   298→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   299→        issues_scoped
   300→    )
   301→    parts = _subjective_summary_parts(
   302→        followup=followup,
   303→        unassessed_subjective=unassessed_subjective,
   304→        subjective_entries=subjective_entries,
   305→        issues_scoped=issues_scoped,
   306→        coverage_open=coverage_open,
   307→    )
   308→    if parts:
   309→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   310→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   311→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""

    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults

    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"

    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""

    66→    @property

    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )

    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """

    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,

   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   118→    plan: PlanModel,
   119→    state: StateModel,
   120→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   121→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   122→
   123→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   124→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   125→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   126→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   127→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   128→    """
   129→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   130→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   131→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   132→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   133→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   134→
   135→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   136→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   137→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   138→        fid for fid in order
   139→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   140→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   141→        and fid not in stale_ids
   142→    ]
   143→    for fid in to_remove:
   144→        order.remove(fid)
   145→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   146→
   147→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   148→    existing = set(order)
   149→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   150→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   151→        if uid not in existing:
   152→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   153→            result.injected.append(uid)
   154→
   155→    return result
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    *,
   166→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   167→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   168→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   169→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   170→
   171→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   172→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   173→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   174→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   175→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   176→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   177→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   178→    """
   179→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   180→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   181→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   182→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   183→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   184→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in injectable_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   200→    if policy is not None:
   201→        has_real_items = policy.has_objective_backlog
   202→    else:
   203→        has_real_items = any(
   204→            f.get("status") == "open"
   205→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   206→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   207→            for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   208→        )
   209→
   210→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   211→
   212→    if not should_inject:
   213→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   214→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   215→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   216→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   217→        to_evict = [
   218→            fid for fid in order
   219→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   220→            and fid in injectable_ids
   221→        ]
   222→        for fid in to_evict:
   223→            order.remove(fid)
   224→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   225→
   226→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   227→        existing = set(order)
   228→        if cycle_just_completed and has_real_items:
   229→            # Post-cycle: front-of-queue after promoted items so subjective
   230→            # review happens before the new objective cycle begins.
   231→            insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   232→            for sid in reversed(sorted(injectable_ids)):
   233→                if sid not in existing:
   234→                    order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   235→                    result.injected.append(sid)
   236→        else:
   237→            # Mid-cycle or no objective backlog: append to back.
   238→            for sid in sorted(injectable_ids):
   239→                if sid not in existing:
   240→                    order.append(sid)
   241→                    result.injected.append(sid)
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
```
