   247→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   248→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249→
   250→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   251→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   252→
   253→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   254→    """
   255→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   256→
   257→
   258→@dataclass
   259→class TriageSyncResult:
   260→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   261→
   262→    injected: bool = False
   263→    pruned: bool = False
   264→
   265→    @property
   266→    def changes(self) -> int:
   267→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   268→
   269→
   270→def sync_triage_needed(
   271→    plan: PlanModel,
   272→    state: StateModel,
   273→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   274→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   275→
   276→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   277→
   278→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   279→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   280→
   281→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   282→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   283→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   284→    """
   285→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   286→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   287→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   288→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   289→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   290→
   291→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   292→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   293→
   294→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→
   297→    if already_present:
   298→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   299→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   300→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   301→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   302→        # user-started triage session.
   303→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   304→            issues = state.get("issues", {})
   305→            current_review_ids = {
   306→                fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   307→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   308→                and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   309→            }
   310→            triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   311→            new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   312→
   313→            if not new_since_triage:
   314→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   315→                for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   316→                    while sid in order:
   317→                        order.remove(sid)
   318→                if current_hash:
   319→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   320→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   321→                result.pruned = True
   322→        return result
   323→
   324→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   325→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   326→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   327→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   328→        current_review_ids = {
   329→            fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   330→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   331→            and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   332→        }
   333→        triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   334→        new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   335→
   336→        if new_since_triage:
   337→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   338→            insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   339→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   340→            existing = set(order)
   341→            injected_count = 0
   342→            for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   343→                if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   344→                    order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   345→                    injected_count += 1
   346→            if injected_count:
   347→                result.injected = True
   348→        else:
   349→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   350→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   351→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   352→
   353→    return result
   354→

   367→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   368→    plan: PlanModel,
   369→    state: StateModel,
   370→    *,
   371→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   372→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   373→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   374→
   375→    Injects when:
   376→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   377→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   378→
   379→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   380→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   381→    """
   382→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   383→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   384→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   385→
   386→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   387→        return result
   388→
   389→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   390→    if policy is not None:
   391→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   392→            return result
   393→    else:
   394→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   395→        if unscored:
   396→            return result
   397→
   398→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   399→    insert_at = 0
   400→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   401→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   402→            insert_at = i + 1
   403→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   404→    result.injected = True
   405→    return result

   419→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   420→    plan: PlanModel,
   421→    state: StateModel,
   422→    *,
   423→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   424→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   425→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   426→
   427→    Only injects when:
   428→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   429→    - At least one objective issue exists
   430→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   431→    - No triage stages are pending
   432→    """
   433→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   434→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   435→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   436→
   437→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   438→        return result
   439→
   440→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   441→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   442→        return result
   443→
   444→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   445→    if policy is not None:
   446→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   447→            return result
   448→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   449→    else:
   450→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   451→        if unscored:
   452→            return result
   453→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   454→        has_objective = any(
   455→            f.get("status") == "open"
   456→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   457→            for f in issues.values()
   458→        )
   459→    if not has_objective:
   460→        return result
   461→
   462→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   463→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   464→    insert_at = 0
   465→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   466→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   467→            insert_at = i + 1
   468→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   469→    result.injected = True
   470→    return result

   481→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   482→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   483→
   484→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   485→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   486→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   487→    resolved since then.
   488→
   489→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   490→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   491→    through the plan.
   492→    """
   493→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   494→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)

   508→def sync_import_scores_needed(

   512→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   513→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   514→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   515→
   516→    Only injects when:
   517→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   518→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   519→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   520→
   521→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   522→    """
   523→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   524→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   525→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   526→
   527→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   528→        return result
   529→
   530→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   531→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   532→        return result
   533→
   534→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   535→    insert_at = 0
   536→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   537→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   538→            insert_at = i + 1
   539→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   540→    result.injected = True
   541→    return result
   542→
   543→
   544→@dataclass
   545→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   546→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   547→
   548→    injected: bool = False
   549→
   550→    @property
   551→    def changes(self) -> int:
   552→        return int(self.injected)
   553→
   554→
   555→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   556→    plan: PlanModel,
   557→    state: StateModel,
   558→    *,
   559→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   560→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   561→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   563→
   564→    Injects when either:
   565→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   566→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   567→
   568→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   569→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   570→    """
   571→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   572→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   573→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   574→
   575→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   576→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   577→        return result
   578→
   579→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   580→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   581→
   582→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   583→    if not should_inject:
   584→        if policy is not None:
   585→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   586→        else:
   587→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   588→
   589→    if not should_inject:
   590→        return result
   591→
   592→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   593→    insert_at = 0
   594→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   595→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   596→            insert_at = i + 1
   597→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   598→    result.injected = True
   599→    return result
   600→
   601→
   602→__all__ = [
   603→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   604→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   605→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   606→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   607→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   608→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   609→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   610→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   611→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   612→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   613→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   614→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   615→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   616→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   617→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",
   618→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   619→    "TriageSyncResult",
   620→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   621→    "current_under_target_ids",
   622→    "current_unscored_ids",
   623→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   624→    "is_triage_stale",
   625→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   626→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   627→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   628→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   629→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   630→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   631→    "sync_triage_needed",
   632→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   633→]

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine import plan as plan_mod
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue import core as work_queue_core_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    17→
    18→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    21→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    22→
    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class QueueBreakdown:
    30→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    31→
    32→    queue_total: int = 0
    33→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    34→    skipped: int = 0
    35→    subjective: int = 0
    36→    workflow: int = 0
    37→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    38→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    39→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    40→
    41→    @property
    42→    def actionable(self) -> int:
    43→        """Items that represent real work — excludes workflow navigation steps.
    44→
    45→        Use this instead of ``queue_total`` when gating on "is there work left?"
    46→        (e.g. scan preflight, plan-start-score clearing).  Workflow items like
    47→        ``workflow::run-scan`` guide the user but are not work to complete.
    48→        """
    49→        return max(0, self.queue_total - self.workflow)
    50→
    51→
    52→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    53→    state: dict,
    54→    plan: dict | None = None,
    55→    *,
    56→    context: QueueContext | None = None,
    57→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    58→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call.
    59→
    60→    When *context* is provided, its ``plan`` and ``policy`` are forwarded to
    61→    ``build_work_queue`` so the counts agree with the caller's resolution.
    62→    """
    63→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else plan
    64→
    65→    result = work_queue_core_mod.build_work_queue(
    66→        state,
    67→        options=work_queue_core_mod.QueueBuildOptions(
    68→            status="open",
    69→            count=None,
    70→            plan=effective_plan if context is None else None,
    71→            include_skipped=False,
    72→            context=context,
    73→        ),
    74→    )
    75→
    76→    # Collapse clusters for display-level counting
    77→    items = result.get("items", [])
    78→    if effective_plan and not effective_plan.get("active_cluster"):
    79→        items = collapse_clusters(items, effective_plan)
    80→
    81→    queue_total = len(items)
    82→
    83→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    84→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    85→    subjective = sum(
    86→        1 for item in items
    87→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    88→    )
    89→    workflow = sum(
    90→        1 for item in items
    91→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    # Plan-derived counts
    95→    plan_ordered = 0
    96→    skipped = 0
    97→    if effective_plan:
    98→        skipped = len(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}))
    99→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
   100→        queue_order = effective_plan.get("queue_order", [])
   101→        skipped_ids = set(effective_plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   102→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
   103→
   104→    # Focus cluster info
   105→    focus_cluster = None
   106→    focus_cluster_count = 0
   107→    focus_cluster_total = 0
   108→    if effective_plan:
   109→        active = effective_plan.get("active_cluster")
   110→        if active:
   111→            focus_cluster = active
   112→            cluster_data = effective_plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   113→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
   115→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   116→            open_issues = {
   117→                fid
   118→                for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   119→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   120→            }
   121→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_issues)
   122→
   123→    return QueueBreakdown(
   124→        queue_total=queue_total,
   125→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
   126→        skipped=skipped,
   127→        subjective=subjective,
   128→        workflow=workflow,
   129→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
   130→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   131→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   136→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   137→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   138→
   139→def format_plan_delta(live: float, frozen: float) -> str:
   140→    """Format plan-start vs live delta, or '' if below threshold."""
   141→    if abs(live - frozen) < 0.05:
   142→        return ""
   143→    delta = round(live - frozen, 1)
   144→    return f"{'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f}"
   145→
   146→
   147→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   148→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   149→
   150→    Examples::
   151→
   152→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   153→        Queue: 1934 items
   154→    """
   155→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   156→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   157→
   158→    # Parenthesized segments
   159→    segments: list[str] = []
   160→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   161→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   162→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   163→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   164→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   165→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   166→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   167→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   168→    if segments:
   169→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   170→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   171→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   172→    return label
   173→
   174→
   175→def format_queue_block(
   176→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   177→    *,
   178→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   179→    live_score: float | None = None,
   180→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   181→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   182→
   183→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   184→    """
   185→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   186→
   187→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   188→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   189→        focus_line = (
   190→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   191→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   192→            f" items remaining"
   193→        )
   194→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   195→
   196→    # Score line: show both frozen plan-start and live score when available
   197→    if frozen_score is not None:
   198→        delta_str = format_plan_delta(live_score, frozen_score) if live_score is not None else ""
   199→        if delta_str:
   200→            lines.append((
   201→                f"  Score: strict {live_score:.1f}/100 (plan start: {frozen_score:.1f}, {delta_str})",
   202→                "cyan",
   203→            ))
   204→        else:
   205→            lines.append((
   206→                f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   207→                "cyan",
   208→            ))
   209→
   210→    # Queue headline — always the same
   211→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   212→
   213→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   214→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   215→        lines.append((
   216→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   217→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   218→            "dim",
   219→        ))
   220→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   221→        lines.append((
   222→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   223→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   224→            "dim",
   225→        ))
   226→    else:
   227→        lines.append((
   228→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   229→            "dim",
   230→        ))
   231→
   232→    return lines
   233→
   234→
   235→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   236→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   237→    if not plan:
   238→        return None
   239→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   240→
   241→
   242→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   243→    plan: dict,
   244→    queue_remaining: int,
   245→    *,
   246→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   247→    live_score: float | None = None,
   248→) -> None:
   249→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
   250→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   251→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   252→    if strict is None:
   253→        return
   254→
   255→    block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict, live_score=live_score)
   256→    print()
   257→    for text, style in block:
   258→        print(colorize(text, style))
   259→    if queue_remaining > 0:
   260→        print(colorize(
   261→            "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   262→            "dim",
   263→        ))
   264→
   265→
   266→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   267→    frozen_strict: float,
   268→    remaining: int,
   269→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   270→) -> None:
   271→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   272→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   273→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   274→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   275→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   276→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   277→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   278→    print(colorize(
   279→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   280→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   281→        "cyan",
   282→    ))
   283→    print(colorize(
   284→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   285→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   286→        "dim",
   287→    ))
   288→
   289→
   290→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   291→    state: dict,
   292→    prev,
   293→    plan: dict | None,
   294→) -> None:
   295→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   296→
   297→    Three display modes:
   298→
   299→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   300→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   301→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   302→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   303→    """
   304→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   305→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   306→
   307→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   308→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   309→        try:
   310→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   311→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   312→            _logger.debug("queue breakdown computation skipped", exc_info=True)
   313→
   314→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   315→
   316→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   317→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   318→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   319→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   320→            # Compute live score for delta display alongside frozen
   321→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   322→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   323→                live_score=state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict,
   324→            )
   325→            return
   326→
   327→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   328→    score_update_mod = importlib.import_module(
   329→        "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update"
   330→    )
   331→    score_update_mod.print_score_update(state, prev)
   332→
   333→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   334→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   335→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   336→
   337→
   338→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   339→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   340→
   341→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   342→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   343→    """
   344→    try:
   345→        plan = plan_mod.load_plan()
   346→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   347→        plan = None
   348→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   349→
   350→
   351→__all__ = [
   352→    "QueueBreakdown",
   353→    "format_plan_delta",
   354→    "format_queue_block",
   355→    "format_queue_headline",
   356→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   357→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   358→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   359→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   360→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   361→]
   362→

    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Lazy import helper — avoids the circular import in resolve/render
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→def _import_guard():
    16→    """Import _check_queue_order_guard, patching away the render dependency."""
    17→    import desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd as cmd_mod
    18→    return cmd_mod._check_queue_order_guard
    19→
    20→
    21→def _get_guard_fn():
    22→    """Return the guard function, handling circular import gracefully."""
    23→    try:
    24→        return _import_guard()
    25→    except ImportError:
    26→        # Fall back: patch the problematic render import chain
    27→        import sys
    28→        stub = MagicMock()
    29→        modules_to_stub = [
    30→            "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update",
    31→            "desloppify.app.commands.scan.helpers",
    32→        ]
    33→        saved = {}
    34→        for mod_name in modules_to_stub:

    36→                saved[mod_name] = sys.modules[mod_name]
    37→            sys.modules[mod_name] = stub
    38→
    39→        # Force reimport

    41→        importlib.reload(cmd_mod)
    42→        fn = cmd_mod._check_queue_order_guard
    43→
    44→        # Restore
    45→        for mod_name in modules_to_stub:
    46→            if mod_name in saved:
    47→                sys.modules[mod_name] = saved[mod_name]
    48→            else:
    49→                sys.modules.pop(mod_name, None)
    50→        return fn
    51→

    77→    import desloppify.engine._plan.persistence as persist_mod

    83→    if clusters:

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _front_item_ids(front_item: dict) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:
    21→    front_id = front_item["id"]
    22→    front_ids = {front_id}
    23→    if front_item.get("kind") != "cluster":
    24→        return front_id, front_ids
    25→    for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    26→        front_ids.add(member["id"])
    27→    return front_id, front_ids
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_target_ids(patterns: list[str], clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    31→    resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    32→    for pattern in patterns:
    33→        if pattern in clusters:
    34→            resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("issue_ids", []))
    35→            resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    36→            continue
    37→        resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    38→    return resolved_ids
    39→
    40→
    41→def _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
    42→    resolved_ids: set[str],
    43→    *,
    44→    clusters: dict,
    45→    issues: dict,
    46→) -> set[str]:
    47→    return {
    48→        issue_id
    49→        for issue_id in resolved_ids
    50→        if issue_id in clusters
    51→        or (issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open")
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def _prune_front_covered_clusters(
    56→    out_of_order: set[str],
    57→    *,
    58→    clusters: dict,
    59→    issues: dict,
    60→    front_ids: set[str],
    61→) -> None:
    62→    for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    63→        if cluster_id not in clusters:
    64→            continue
    65→        alive_members = {
    66→            issue_id
    67→            for issue_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open"
    69→        }
    70→        if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    71→            out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_queue_order_violation(front_id: str, out_of_order: set[str]) -> None:
    75→    print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    76→    for issue_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    77→        print(f"    {issue_id}")
    78→    print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    79→    print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan reorder <pattern> top            # move to front", "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    82→    print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    patterns: list[str],
    88→    status: str,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    91→    if status != "fixed":
    92→        return False
    93→    try:
    94→        if not has_living_plan():
    95→            return False
    96→        plan = load_plan()
    97→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    98→        if not queue_order:
    99→            return False
   100→
   101→        ctx = queue_context(state, plan=plan)
   102→        result = build_work_queue(
   103→            state,
   104→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   105→                count=None,
   106→                include_subjective=True,
   107→                context=ctx,
   108→            ),
   109→        )
   110→        items = result["items"]
   111→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   113→        if not items:
   114→            return False
   115→
   116→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   117→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→        resolved_ids = _resolve_target_ids(patterns, clusters)
   119→        resolved_ids = _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
   120→            resolved_ids,
   121→            clusters=clusters,
   122→            issues=issues,
   123→        )
   124→        if not resolved_ids:
   125→            return False
   126→
   127→        # Collect IDs from the first N queue positions where N is the
   128→        # number of items being resolved.  This allows batch-resolving
   129→        # a contiguous prefix (e.g. the first 9 items in one command).
   130→        prefix_depth = max(len(resolved_ids), 1)
   131→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
   132→        front_id = items[0]["id"]
   133→        for item in items[:prefix_depth]:
   134→            _, item_ids = _front_item_ids(item)
   135→            front_ids.update(item_ids)
   136→
   137→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
   138→        _prune_front_covered_clusters(
   139→            out_of_order,
   140→            clusters=clusters,
   141→            issues=issues,
   142→            front_ids=front_ids,
   143→        )
   144→        if not out_of_order:
   145→            return False
   146→
   147→        _print_queue_order_violation(front_id, out_of_order)
   148→        return True
   149→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   150→        _logger.debug("queue order guard skipped", exc_info=True)
   151→        return False
   152→
   153→
   154→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
   155→

    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Dimension key normalization
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }
    57→
    58→

    63→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:

    68→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)

    70→    for entry in entries:

    72→        if not name:
    73→            continue
    74→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    75→        [REDACTED](name)
    76→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    77→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    78→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    79→            if not key:
    80→                continue
    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores
    88→

    93→
    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→
   165→
   166→def build_subjective_items(
   167→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   168→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   169→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   170→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   171→    if not dim_scores:
   172→        return []
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   174→
   175→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   176→    if not subjective_entries:
   177→        return []
   178→    unassessed_dims = {
   179→        str(name).strip()
   180→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   181→            dim_scores
   182→        )
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   186→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   187→    for issue in issues.values():
   188→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   189→            continue
   190→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   191→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   192→            if dim_key:
   193→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   194→
   195→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   196→    def _prepare_command(
   197→        cli_keys: list[str],
   198→        *,
   199→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   200→    ) -> str:
   201→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   202→        if cli_keys:
   203→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   204→        if force_review_rerun:
   205→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   206→        return command
   207→
   208→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   209→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   210→        if not name:
   211→            continue
   212→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   213→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   214→            continue
   215→
   216→        [REDACTED](name)
   217→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   218→        cli_keys = [
   219→            str(key).strip().lower()
   220→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   221→            if str(key).strip()
   222→        ]
   223→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   224→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   225→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   226→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   227→            name in unassessed_dims
   228→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   229→        )
   230→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   231→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   232→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   233→        if open_review > 0:
   234→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   235→        else:
   236→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   237→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   238→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   239→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   240→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   241→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   242→            "file": ".",
   243→            "confidence": "medium",
   244→            "summary": summary,
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "dimension_name": name,
   247→                "dimension": dim_key,
   248→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   249→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   250→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   251→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   252→            },
   253→            "status": "open",
   254→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   255→        }
   256→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   257→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   258→        items.append(item)
   259→    return items
   260→
   261→
   262→__all__ = [
   263→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   264→    "build_create_plan_item",

Here is a complete assessment of each detector relative to the reported false positive issues.

---

## 1. `async_no_await` detector (issue #190)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/detectors/_smell_detectors.py`, lines 180-195

**Implementation:**

```python
def _detect_async_no_await(ctx: _FileContext, smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
    async_re = re.compile(r"(?:async\s+function\s+(\w+)|(\w+)\s*=\s*async)")
    for i, line in enumerate(ctx.lines):
        m = async_re.search(line)
        if not m:
            continue
        name = m.group(1) or m.group(2)
        body = _extract_function_body(ctx.lines, i)
        if body is not None and not re.search(r"\bawait\b", _code_text(body)):
            _emit(smell_counts, "async_no_await", ctx, i + 1, ...)
```

**Issue #190 concerns addressed:**

- **Multi-line function body**: RESOLVED. The detector uses `_extract_function_body()` backed by `_track_brace_body()`, which walks brace depth with string-literal awareness across all lines (up to a 2000-line scan limit). A former 200-line cap has been explicitly removed — this is documented by the test `test_await_beyond_line_200_detected`, which places `await` at line 250+ and confirms it is no longer missed.

- **`await` in comments counted as real**: RESOLVED. `_code_text(body)` strips comments and string literals to spaces before the `\bawait\b` search is run. Tests `test_await_in_comment_still_flagged` and `test_await_in_string_still_flagged` confirm these false negatives no longer occur.

- **Text/regex vs AST**: Still regex-based — not a tree-sitter/AST pass. However the body extraction is brace-tracked with `scan_code` (string-literal-aware), not naive line-counting. This is an improvement over pure text scanning but not full AST.

**Assessment:** The 70% false positive concern (missing `await` in multi-line functions) appears fully addressed by the current implementation.

---

## 2. `dead_function` detector (issue #191)

This detector exists in two separate language implementations. **There is no `dead_function` detector for TypeScript at all.** The TypeScript unused-symbol detection is a completely separate mechanism via `unused.py`, which delegates to `tsc --noUnusedLocals` (the TypeScript compiler's own static analysis) for full cross-file usage tracking.

**For Python**, the detector is in:

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/detectors/smells_ast/_node_detectors.py`, lines 35-67

```python
def _detect_dead_functions(filepath: str, node: ast.AST) -> list[dict]:
    """Flag functions whose body is only pass, return, or return None."""
    if node.decorator_list:
        return []
    body = node.body
    if len(body) == 1:
        stmt = body[0]
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass) or _is_return_none(stmt):
            return [...]
    elif len(body) == 2:
        first, second = body
        if not _is_docstring(first):
            return []
        if isinstance(second, ast.Pass):
            ...
        elif _is_return_none(second):
            ...
```

**Issue #191 concerns addressed:**

This is a critical clarification: **"dead_function" in this codebase does not mean "unreferenced function."** It means a function whose _body is a stub_ — only `pass`, `return`, `return None`, or a docstring followed by one of those. It does **not** attempt call-graph analysis, cross-file import tracking, or callback reference detection.

- **No call-graph analysis**: Correct — not attempted, and not the intent of this detector. The detector's actual semantic is "stub body," not "unreachable symbol."
- **Decorated functions skipped**: Yes — `if node.decorator_list: return []` prevents false positives on abstract methods, property stubs, protocol stubs, etc.
- **AST-based**: Yes, fully AST-based using Python's `ast` module. No regex.

**Issue #191 framing of "97% false positive rate" for cross-codebase dead code** does not match what this detector actually does. If issue #191 was expecting call-graph-based dead code detection, that is a different capability (similar to how the TypeScript side uses `tsc` for `detect_unused`). The Python `dead_function` detector intentionally catches only structural stubs.

---

## 3. `console_error_no_throw` detector (issue #192)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/detectors/_smell_detectors.py`, lines 337-358 (function `_detect_error_no_throw`)

```python
_ERROR_HANDLER_BASENAMES = ("logger", "errorpresentation", "errorhandler", "errorreporting")

_PRECEDING_SKIP_PATTERNS = re.compile(
    r"componentDidCatch|import\.meta\.env\.DEV|process\.env\.NODE_ENV"
)

_HANDLED_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:throw|return)\b|toast\(|normalizeAndPresentError\(|presentError\(|rethrow"
)

def _detect_error_no_throw(ctx, smell_counts):
    basename_no_ext = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(ctx.filepath).lower())[0]
    if any(tag in basename_no_ext for tag in _ERROR_HANDLER_BASENAMES):
        return
    for index, line in enumerate(ctx.lines):
        if "console.error" not in line:
            continue
        preceding = "\n".join(ctx.lines[max(0, index - 10) : index])
        if _PRECEDING_SKIP_PATTERNS.search(preceding):
            continue
        following = "\n".join(ctx.lines[index + 1 : index + 4])
        if not _HANDLED_RE.search(following):
            _emit(smell_counts, "console_error_no_throw", ...)
```

**Issue #192 concerns addressed:**

- **Error boundary exclusion**: RESOLVED. `componentDidCatch` in the 10 preceding lines suppresses the flag. Test `test_skips_component_did_catch` confirms this.

- **Logger/utility file exclusion**: RESOLVED. Files whose basename (without extension) contains `logger`, `errorpresentation`, `errorhandler`, or `errorreporting` are skipped entirely. Tests `test_skips_logger_files` and `test_skips_error_handler_files` confirm this.

- **Dev-only block exclusion**: RESOLVED. `import.meta.env.DEV` and `process.env.NODE_ENV` in the 10-line preceding context suppress the flag. Test `test_skips_dev_only_block` confirms this.

- **`return` counts as handled**: Yes — `_HANDLED_RE` includes `\breturn\b`, so `console.error` followed by an early return is not flagged.

- **`toast()` / custom presenters count as handled**: Yes — `toast(`, `normalizeAndPresentError(`, `presentError(`, and `rethrow` are all in `_HANDLED_RE`. Test `test_toast_counts_as_handled` confirms this.

**Remaining limitations:** The "following 3 lines" lookahead window is narrow. A `throw` on line 5 after the `console.error` would still be flagged. The `_ERROR_HANDLER_BASENAMES` set is an exact-substring list that would miss files like `errorUtils.ts` or `reportError.ts`. These are potential sources of remaining false positives, but the main categories from issue #192 are addressed.

---

## Summary Table

| Issue | Detector | Root Concern | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| #190 | `async_no_await` | Missing `await` in multi-line functions; `await` in comments counted | **Addressed** — brace-tracked body extraction with 2000-line scan; `_code_text()` strips comments/strings before `await` search |
| #191 | `dead_function` | Cross-codebase call graph, callback refs, imports | **Mismatch of intent** — detector intentionally flags stub bodies only, not unreferenced symbols; decorators are excluded; TS uses compiler-level `tsc` for true unused detection |
| #192 | `console_error_no_throw` | Error boundaries, logger files, dev-only blocks | **Addressed** — file-basename exclusions, `componentDidCatch` preceding-context skip, `DEV`/`NODE_ENV` skip, `toast()`/custom handlers in handled-RE |

## Issue Status Report

### Issue #193 — wontfix findings in queue

**Status: Addressed.**

The concern was that wontfix (permanent-skip) findings appearing in `queue_order` would pollute the queue when `queue_order` was otherwise empty.

What the code does now:
- `stamp_plan_sort_keys` in `plan_order.py` (lines 66-72) already excludes skipped IDs from the `position_map`. Skipped entries (including permanent wontfix) are stripped from the active position mapping before sort keys are stamped.
- `separate_skipped` in `plan_order.py` (lines 81-99) physically separates skipped items from the non-skipped list before sorting. They are only re-appended when `include_skipped=True` is explicitly passed.
- The test `test_guard_allows_non_fixed_status` in `test_queue_order_guard.py` confirms that resolving with status `wontfix` bypasses the queue guard entirely (line 120-121).
- The queue order guard itself (`queue_guard.py` line 91) returns `False` immediately when `status != "fixed"`, so wontfix resolutions are never blocked regardless of queue position.

The wontfix findings are separated from the queue before ordering takes effect, and wontfix resolutions bypass the guard. This issue is resolved.

---

### Issue #182 — plan deadlock with stale findings

**Status: Addressed.**

The concern was that stale (no longer open) IDs at the front of `queue_order` would block resolution of live findings via the head-of-queue check.

The fix is in `queue_guard.py`'s `_check_queue_order_guard`. Instead of comparing against a raw `queue_order` prefix, it:
1. Calls `build_work_queue` with `count=None` (line 102-109), which only returns currently-open items.
2. Compares the resolved target IDs against the live queue front — not against raw `queue_order`.
3. Uses `_filter_open_or_cluster_targets` (lines 41-52) to filter the patterns being resolved to only open issues or existing clusters. Stale IDs that resolve to nothing (not in issues or clusters with status=open) drop out and can never be "out of order."

The tests in `test_queue_order_guard.py` explicitly cover this (lines 212-267):
- `test_guard_skips_stale_ids_in_queue_order` — stale ID at front of queue_order does not block live item `b`.
- `test_guard_skips_resolved_ids_in_queue_order` — fixed (non-open) ID does not block the next live item.
- `test_guard_cluster_with_stale_members` — cluster with a stale member does not deadlock.
- `test_guard_all_resolved_ids_stale` — all-stale IDs at front: no blocking.

This issue is fully resolved.

---

### Issue #184/185/186 — stale subjective reruns surfacing before objective drain; plan ordering bypasses impact ranking

**Status: Addressed.**

**Stale subjective reruns (Issues #184, #185):**

The key mechanism is `_apply_lifecycle_filter` in `core.py` (lines 290-299). It filters out all `subjective_dimension` items that are not `initial_review` when any objective mechanical items remain. This means stale reassessment items cannot surface in the queue until the objective backlog is drained.

`sync_stale_dimensions` in `stale_dimensions.py` (lines 162-243) enforces this at the plan queue level too: when `has_real_items` is True and `cycle_just_completed` is False, it actively evicts stale/under-target IDs from `queue_order` (lines 212-224).

`sync_subjective_clusters` in `auto_cluster_sync.py` (lines 285-318) applies the same gating: the `auto/under-target-review` cluster is only created when `not has_objective_items`, and existing under-target IDs are evicted from the queue when objective work remains (lines 311-317).

**Impact-based ordering bypasses (Issue #186):**

`_natural_sort_key` in `ranking.py` (lines 189-238) uses `estimated_impact` as the primary sort field (negated, so higher impact sorts first) for both individual issues and subjective dimensions. The `enrich_with_impact` function (lines 67-96) computes impact as `overall_per_point * headroom` where headroom = `100 - score`, so dimensions with more room to improve sort first.

`item_sort_key` (lines 241-264) adds plan-position overrides on top: items with a `_plan_position` sort first in plan order. But that is intentional — if the user explicitly placed something in queue_order, their ordering overrides natural impact ranking. Items not in queue_order use natural impact ranking.

These issues are resolved.

---

### Issue #187 — diminishing returns awareness

**Status: Partially addressed.**

There is an impact floor mechanism in `core.py`: `_passes_impact_floor` (lines 227-234) with `_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05`. Mechanical issues with `estimated_impact < 0.05` are dropped from the queue entirely. This handles the case of "items in a dimension that is already at or near 100 have negligible impact and should not clutter the queue."

The test `test_impact_floor_filters_negligible_impact` in `work_queue_cases.py` (line 803) confirms this with a comment: "Impact is near-zero because dimension is at ceiling."

However, there is no explicit "ceiling detection" or progressive warning that communicates diminishing returns to the user. The impact floor silently removes negligible items. There is no banner, nudge, or message along the lines of "this dimension is near its ceiling; further work will yield diminishing returns." The `_render_detector_impact_estimate` renderer in `render.py` (lines 156-178) does show a per-issue impact estimate in points, which gives the user implicit signal, but there is no proactive ceiling detection module or threshold-based explanation.

**Summary: the queue is cleaned up by the impact floor (preventing truly negligible items from appearing), but there is no explicit user-facing communication about diminishing returns as a concept.**

---

### Issue #200 — score progress not communicated after review import

**Status: Addressed.**

Multiple mechanisms now communicate score progress after review import:

1. **`show_score_with_plan_context`** is called directly in the review import command (`importing/cmd.py` line 502). This shows either the frozen plan-start score (if objective queue items remain) or the live updated scores.

2. **`workflow::communicate-score`** synthetic queue item (implemented in `synthetic_workflow.py` lines 95-129). When scores are just imported (`scores_just_imported=True`) or all initial reviews complete, `sync_communicate_score_needed` injects this item into `queue_order`. It surfaces in `next` as a workflow action with the current strict score and delta from plan start in the summary: `"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}"`.

3. **`workflow::score-checkpoint`** (the original mechanism, lines 8-39): injected after all initial reviews complete, shows current strict score and delta.

4. **`workflow::import-scores`** (lines 64-92): injected when issues-only import was done without scores, guides the user to re-import with attestation.

5. The `_render_frozen_queue_status` and `_render_north_star` functions in `render_nudges.py` show the frozen plan-start score alongside the live score at every `next` call when an active plan exists, explicitly showing the gap to target.

The issue is fully resolved. Score progress is surfaced both immediately on import (via `show_score_with_plan_context`) and persistently via workflow queue items that guide the user to acknowledge and communicate the updated score.

---

## Summary Table

| Issue | Title | Status |
|---|---|---|
| #193 | wontfix findings in queue | Resolved — skipped IDs excluded from position map; wontfix status bypasses guard |
| #182 | plan deadlock with stale findings | Resolved — guard uses live `build_work_queue` result, not raw `queue_order`; four explicit tests |
| #184/#185 | stale subjective reruns before objective drain | Resolved — `_apply_lifecycle_filter` + `sync_stale_dimensions` eviction enforces drain-first |
| #186 | plan ordering bypasses impact ranking | Resolved — impact used as primary natural sort key; plan overrides are explicit user intent |
| #187 | diminishing returns awareness | Partially addressed — impact floor silently drops negligible items, but no explicit ceiling communication to user |
